e-ṭu: uttama cōḻat terinta antaḷa kattāḷar   (S.I.I.ii,97)
antaḷakam meymmaṟai (kavacam)
iṉi, vēntaṉ mēl aḷaviṟanta paṟṟuṭaiyavarāy, avaṉukkut tuṉpam nērnta vēḷaiyil uyiraikkoṭuttuk kākkavum uṭaṉ māyavum cūḷiṭṭuk koṇṭa pōr maṟavarumiruntaṉar. avar vēḷaikkārar eṉappaṭṭār. avar uyir koṭuttal ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭaṉ kaḻittal eṉappaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ, allatu tuṟantapiṉ, allatu kaḻiperu mūppaṭaintapiṉ, avaṉ mūtta makaṉ, mūtta makaṉ illāviṭṭāl iḷaiya makaṉ. makaṉē illāviṭṭāl makaḷ, makaḷum illā viṭṭāl takutiyuḷḷa neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ, muṭicūṭṭappaṭṭār. taṭātakai yeṉṉum kayaṟkaṇṇiyār pāṇṭināṭṭai yāṇṭataiyum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār cēranāṭṭai yāṇṭataiyum kāṇka. muṭicūṭṭu viḻāvilum vēntaṉ piṟanta nāḷākiya veḷḷaṇi viḻāvilum, piṟa ciṟanta nikaḻccikaḷilum, kōppēruntēviyum uṭaṉ koluviruppāḷ.
kāviti eṉpatu ciṟanta amaiccaṉukkum, ēṉāti eṉpatu ciṟanta paṭait talaivaṉukkum, vēḷ allatu piḷḷai eṉpatu ciṟanta kuṟunila maṉṉarukkum, māvaraiyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟanta araciyaṟ karumat talaivaṉukkum, eṭṭi eṉpatu ciṟanta vaṇikaṉukkum, ciṟutaṉam, peruntaṉam, talaikkōl eṉpaṉa nāṭakak kaṇikaiyarkkum, vēntaṉ aḷikkum paṭṭaṅkaḷākum.
ēṉai + ari = ēṉāri (yāṉaikaḷai aḻippavaṉ) - ēṉāti. ta - ra oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu pōliyāka varum. e-ṭu: vitai - virai, kural vaḷai - kutavaḷai(koccaiyulaka vaḻakku). māvaraiyaṉ -māvaraiyam - mārāyam.
mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yāṉum                     (tol.puṟat.8)
araciyal viṉaiñar ūtiyattiṟkuk kaimmāṟākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu, nellum poṉṉumāyiṉ campaḷam eṉṟum, nilamāṉiya māyiṉ umpaḷam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
13. kalvi
kalvi oru kulattarkku maṭṭum eṉṟu varaiyaṟukkappaṭātu, ellāt toḻil vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. itai,
	tantai makaṟ kāṟṟum naṉṟi yavaiyattu
	munti yiruppac ceyal.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷālum (67)
	īṉṟu puṟan tarutal eṉṟalaik kaṭaṉē
	cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantaikkuk kaṭaṉē
	vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
	oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
	kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷaikkuk kaṭaṉē.	             (puṟam.312)
	vēṟṟumai terinta nāṟpā luḷḷum
	kīḻppāl oruvaṉ kaṟpiṉ
	mēṟpā loruvaṉum avaṉ kaṭ paṭumē.                    (puṟam.183)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭukkaḷālum, palvēṟu toḻilār kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattup pulavarāyiruntamaiyālum, aṟiyalām.
kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalaic cāttaṉār, poṉvāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār, aṟuvai vāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār, iḷam poṉ vaṇikaṉār, maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār, taṇkāṟpūṭ kollaṉār, olaikkaṭaik kaṇṇam pukuntārāyattaṉār, vaṭama vaṇṇakkaṉ peruñcāttaṉār mutaliya pulavar palvēṟu toḻilārā yiruntamai kāṇka.
kalvi peṇṭirkku vilakkappaṭavillai. okkūr mācāttiyar, auvaiyār, kākkai pāṭiṉiyār, nacceḷḷaiyār, kāvaṟpeṇṭu, kuṟamakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi. nakkaṇṇaiyār, pāri makaḷir, pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, pēymakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, māṟōkkattu nappacalaiyār, veṇṇik 
kuyattiyār, veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār mutaliyōr peyar veḷivanta kaṭaikkaḻakak kālap pulattiyar. akkālattuk kuṭittaṉap peṇṭir tam kalviyaṟivai yellām illaṟattiṟkē payaṉpaṭuttiyamaiyāl. avaruṭ perum pālār peyar veḷi varavillai. kāraikkālammaiyār ciṟanta pulattiyarāyiruntum, kaṇavarāl vilakkappaṭumvarai, avar pāvaṉmai veḷippaṭātiruntamai kāṇka. civappirakāca aṭikaḷ kālamāṉa patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tirukkāṭṭuppaḷḷiyil teruvil uppu viṟkum peṇ orutti, aṭikaḷ niṟaiya vuḷatō eṉṟu pāṭi viṉaviya veṇpāviṟku viṭaiyāka,
	teṉṉōṅku tillaic civappirakācapperumāṉ
	poṉṉōṅku cēvaṭiyaip pōṟṟiṉōm - aṉṉōṉ
	tirukkūṭṭam attaṉaikkum teṇṭaṉiṭṭōm tīrāk
	karukkūṭṭam pōkkiṉōm kāṇ.

	piṟṟainilai muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟē.

kaṟpippōr kaṇakkāyaṉ eṉṟum āciriyaṉ eṉṟum kuru allatu kuravaṉ eṉṟum mūvakaiyar. kaṇakkāyaṉ eḻuttum ciṟṟilakkiyamum uriccollum(nikaṇṭum) kaṇakkum kaṟpippōṉ; āciriyaṉ, piṟkālattil aintāka virikkappaṭṭa mūvilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṟkeṭuttuk kāṭṭāṉa pērilakkiyamum kaṟpippōṉ; kuravaṉ camaya nūlum paṭṭāṅku (tattuva) nūlum kaṟpippōṉ.

kaṟkum iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷi (tiv. periyati. 2,3,8) eṉṟum, ōtum paḷḷi (tivā) eṉṟum, kallūri (cīvaka.995) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.

kaṟṟōrkkuc ceṉṟaviṭamellām ciṟappiruntatu. vēntaraip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pulavarkkuc ciṟanta paricum muṟṟūṭṭum (carva māṉiyamum) aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pāṇṭiyar talaiciṟanta pulavarai yellāṅkūṭṭi, tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuvip pōṟṟiṉar.
pulavariṉ pāṭalkaḷum nūlkaḷum, pāṇṭiyar tamiḻk kaḻakattil kuṟṟamaṟṟavaiyeṉa oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭāloḻiya, ulakil vaḻaṅkā. aṅṅaṉam oppam peṟutal araṅkēṟṟam eṉappaṭṭatu.
nāṭakak kaṇikaiyariṉ āṭal pāṭalkaḷum vēntar muṉṉilaiyilēyē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. araṅkēṟiya kaṇikaiyar talaikkōṟpaṭṭamum āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu paricamum peṟṟaṉar.
ittakaiya araṅkēṟṟa muṟaiyāl, akkālattuk kalvi talaiciṟan

pōr purintu tōṟkaṭittut taṇṭittu aṟivu pukaṭṭuvatum akkāla vaḻakkam.

tākum. kallutal tōṇṭutal, māntaṉ mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavut toḻilātalāl, kalvi eṉṉum col mutalil uḻavut toḻilaiyē kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. āṅkilattil nilap paṇpāṭṭaiyum uḷappaṇpāṭṭaiyum oruṅkē uṇarttum Culture, Cultivation eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum, Col eṉṉum orē ilattīṉ vērc colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu. kal- Col - Culture.
14.kalaikaḷ
payiṟciyāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa kalaikaḷ.
(1) icai
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar nuṇmāṇnuḻai pulattarāyum talaiciṟanta nākarikarāyum eḵkuc ceviyarāyu miruntatiṉāl,ēḻu pēricaiyum aintu ciṟṟicaiyumākiya paṉṉīricaiyai (curattai)yum kaṇṭu āyappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil eḻupālaip paṇkaḷait tirittatumaṉṟi, appaṉṉīricaiyaiyum vaṭṭappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 24 ākavum, tirikōṇappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 48 ākavum caturappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 96 ākavum, nuṭpamāyp pakuttu ellaiyaṟṟa icaip pēriṉpattai nukarntirukkiṉaṟaṉar. paṉṉīricaikku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇṇicaiyiṉai eṭuttuk kūṟum nūlē icai nuṇukkam nghY«! ēḻicaiyum kural, tuttam, kaikkaḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟṟicai ākaṇam eṉṟum, kuralum iḷiyumallāta pēricai antaram eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
tiri, caturam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkappeṟum. catur eṉpatu, nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup poruḷilaṉṟi, nāṟkōṇam eṉṉum vaṭivupporuḷil vaṭamoḻi yilillai.
akanilai marutam, puṟanilai marutam, arukiyal marutam, perukiyal marutam eṉṉum paṇ vakuppum, kiḻamai niṟaikuṟai eṉpaṉavum, tamiḻariṉ icai nuṇukkattaik kāṭṭum.
ikkālattu āriya icaiyaṟiñar, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ aṟivu nuṇukkattai ōrātu, tampōl avaraiyuṅ karuti 96 icaikaḷ pāṭaṟkiyalātavai eṉpar.
icaik karuvikaḷ tōl, tuḷai, narampu, veṇkalam (kañcalam) eṉa nālvakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa.
tōṟ karuvikaḷ āṭal muḻā, pāṭal muḻā, potu muḻā eṉṟum; akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇamai muḻavu, nāḷ muḻavu, kālai muḻavu, eṉṟum; maṇappaṟai, piṇappaṟai eṉṟum; vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. paṟai eṉpatu tōṟkaruvip potup peyar. teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟai(tol.964).
iṉṉicait tuḷaik karuvikaḷ pullāṅkuḻal, icaik kuḻal (nātacuram), mukavīṇai eṉṉum ēḻicaik kuḻalkaḷākavum; ottu (ūmaik kuḻal), eṉṉum ōricaik kuḻalākavum; iruvakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa. tārai, ciṉṉam, vāṅkā, kompu, caṅku mutaliya oricait tuḷaik karuvikaḷ, ūrvalam pōr mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷil āravāra icaik karuvikaḷāyp payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. makuṭi eṉpatu nalla pāmpai mayakkutaṟkeṉṟē amaikkappaṭṭa ēḻicaic ciṟappiṉṉicaik kuḻalākum.
narappuk karuvikaḷ oru narampuḷḷa curaiyāḻiliruntu āyiram narampukaḷ peruṅkalam (ātiyāḻ) varai, palvēṟu vakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅ kōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum nāṉkum ciṟantavai. avaṟṟuḷḷum talaiciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ. atuvē piṉṉar vīṇaiyākat tirintatu. āyiram narampeṉpatu, āyiraṅkāṟ pūcci eṉpatiṟpōl, narampiṉ peruntokaiyaiyē kuṟikkum.
``iṉṉicai vīṇaiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl eṉṉum tiruvācakak kūṟṟu (369) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇaiyeṉṟu ciṟappup peyar peṟṟamaiyaiyē kāṭṭum. ellā narappukruvikaḷum yāḻē. vīṇai eṉṉum peyarum viṇ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉ collē. atu vaṭamoḻiyil vīṇā eṉṟu tiriyum. viṇṇeṉal narampu teṟittolittal. viṇ - vīṇai mutukuṉṟam (paḻamalai) viruttācala meṉṟa peyar māṟiyatāl vēṟu nakaramākiviṭātu. aṅṅaṉamē vīṇaiyeṉap paṭum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum eṉka.
iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cilappatikārattil nārataṉ vīṇai nayanteri pāṭalum eṉṟum, 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa appar tēvārattil mācil vīṇaiyum eṉṟum, varutalāl, vīṇai11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpatu poruntātu. vētakāla nāratar tamiḻnāṭu vantē icaikaṟṟup pañcapāra tīyam eṉṉum icait tamiḻ nūlai iyaṟṟiṉār. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cīvakacintāmaṇik kāntaruvatattaiyilampakattil, yāḻeṉṟum vīṇaiyeṉṟum orē karuvi kuṟikkappaṭuvatāl, yāḻ vēṟu vīṇai vēṟu alla.  ikkālattil curaiyāḻum vīṇaiyeṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tiruñāṉa campantar pāṭiya yāḻmurip paṇṇiṉ iyalpu iṉṟu evarukkum teriyāmaiyāl, yāḻmuri vīṇaikkamaiyum eṉpatum poruntātu.
tāḷak karuvi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyapṭṭatiṉāl, atu veṇkalak karuviyeṉappaṭṭatu. cal allatu callarai eṉṉum ōcaiyuṭaimaiyāl tāḷak karuviyiṟ periyatu callariyeṉṉum ciṟiyatu cālar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kural eṉpatu ma eṉṉum 5-ām icaiyeṉṟum, oru narampil orēyicai yeḻūum narappuk karuviyē tamiḻaratu eṉṟum, kūṟuvār tamiḻarāyiṉum icait tamiḻ aṭippaṭaiyē aṟiyātār āvar. ``kural mutalēḻum'' eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap.5: 35), ``mutaṟṟāṉa mākiya kuralilē eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟu uraiyāciriyarum (puṟam.11), mūvēḻ tuṟaiyum muṟaiyuḷik kaḻippi eṉṟu vaṉparaṇarum (puṟam.152), kūṟiyiruttalaiyum, avar kavaṉittilar.
kural eṉṉum collē, iyalpāṉa kuralākiya mutalicaiyaiyē kuṟikkum. curaiyāḻum peruṅkalamumē vilyāḻ (Harp)nghš meṭṭiṉṟi ōricaik koru narampu koṇṭavai. pēyāḻ mutaliya piṟayāḻellām meṭṭukkaḷōṭukūṭi, orē narampil palavicaiyicaik kakkūṭiya paṇmoḻi narampukaḷ koṇṭaṉavākavē terikiṉṟaṉa.
paṇkaḷai, ēḻicaiyumuḷḷavai paṇ eṉṟum, āṟicai yuḷḷavai paṇṇiyal eṉṟum, aiyicaiyuḷḷavai tiṟam eṉṟum, nālicaiyuḷḷavai tiṟattiṟam eṉṟum, nālvakaiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar.  innālvakaip paṇkaḷum mottam 11,991 eṉak kaṇikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.

paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai, icaippāṇarum yāḻp pāṇarum kuḻaṟpāṇarum maṇṭaip pāṇaramāṉa nālvakaip pāṇarē perumpālum tamiḻ nāṭṭil icaivāṇarāyiruntu tamiḻicaiyaip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. pāṇ cēriyil pāṭṭup pāṭukiṟātā?. eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, tirumuṟaikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapil vanta oru peṇṇaik koṇṭu tēvārattiṟku icai vakuppittamaiyum, itaivaliyuṟuttum. āriyak kulappiriviṉai ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ,pāṇar tīṇṭātavārākit tam tolvaravup pāṇtoḻilai iḻantaṉar. eḻūum icai tokaiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum.

	narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl(33), tamiḻicaiyiṉ yāḻc ciṟappu uṇarappaṭum. itaṉ virivai eṉ `muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.
(2) nāṭakam
nāṭakam eṉpatu teṉcollē. naḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal. naḷ - naḷi eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu. naḷital ottal.naḷi - naṭi o.nō: kaḷiṟu - kaṭiṟu. naṭittal eṉpatu, iṉṉoruvaṉaip pōl allatu illātatai uḷḷatu pōlac ceytu kāṭṭutal.
naṭi - naṭam - naṭṭam - nrutta (va.)
o.nō: vaṭṭam - vrutta (va.)
naṭṭam - naṭṭaṇam, naṭṭaṇai
naṭṭam - naṭṭa(pirā)
o.nō: vaṭṭam - vaṭṭaṇam, vaṭṭaṇai
naṭṭaṇam - narttaṉa (va.)
naṭṭam - naṭṭuvaṉ. o.nō: kuṭṭam - kuṭṭuvaṉ
naṭi - naṭaṉam. o.nō: paṭi - paṭaṉam = paṭippu
naṭi - nāṭakam. o.nō: paṭi - pāṭakam = pātattiṟ paṭinta kiṭakkum aṇi.
muttamiḻ toṉṟutoṭṭa iyalicai nāṭakam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkum.
nāṭakak kalai kūttu, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kutittāṭuvatu kūttu. atu vēttiyal, potuviyal; ulakiyal, tēviyal; vacaik kūttu, pukaḻk kūttu; varikkūttu, variyamaitik kūttu (variccāntik kūttu); amaitik kūttu (cāntik kūttu), vēṭikkaik kūttu(vinōtak kūttu); akak kūttu, puṟak kūttu, viḷaiyāṭṭuk kūttu, viṉaik kūttu; veṟṟikkūttu, tōlvik kūttu; eṉappalvēṟu vakaiyil ivviruvakaippaṭum.
naṭaṉam allatu naṭam eṉpatu, aḻakuṟa āṭuvatu.  atu nūṟṟeṭṭu uṭaṟkaraṇaṅkaḷōṭum kaikāl kaṇvāy mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷōṭum kūṭiyatu. kaiviṉaikaḷ eḻiṟkai toḻiṟkai, poruṭkai eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭu,piṇṭi allatu iṇaiyā viṉaikkai yeṉappaṭum oṟṟaikkai vaṇṇam muppattu mūṉṟum, piṇaiyal allatu iṇaikkai yeṉappaṭum iraṭṭaikkai vaṇṇam patiṉaintum, koṇṭaṉavākum.
naṭam naṭaṉam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil ,naṭṭa naṭṭaṉa eṉṟu valikkum. naṭṭa eṉpatiṉiṉṟum nāṭya eṉṉum coṟ piṟakkum.
naṭi eṉṉum mutaṉilai vaṭamoḻiyil illai. nirutta eṉṉum colliṉ nrut eṉṉum aṭiyaiyē mutaṉilaiyāka āḷvar.
tamiḻ naṭaṉam iṉṟu parata nāṭṭiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. parata cāttiram vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu  ki.mu 4ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭākum. ataṟkum muntiyatu tamiḻp paratamē yeṉpatai, nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiya mutalāvuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷu miṟantaṉa, eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāravuraip pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟintu koḷka.
nāṭakam eṉpatu katai taḻuvivarum kūttu. atu poruḷ, katai (yōṉi), talaimai (virutti), nilai (canti), cuvai, vakuppu (cāti), kuṟippu, viṟal (cattuvam), naḷinayam (apinayam), col, colvakai, vaṇṇam, vari,cētam eṉṉum patiṉāṉ kuṟuppukkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.
nāṭaka araṅku nalla keṭṭi nilattil, īraṭi nīḷamuḷḷakōlāl, eṇkōl nīḷamum eḻukōl akalamum orukōl uyaramum uḷḷatāka amaikkappaṭṭu,mēlē mukaṭum, orumuka veḻiṉi porumuka veḻiṉi karantuvara leḻiṉi eṉṉum mūvakait ticaikaḷum, pukuvāyil puṟappaṭa
vāyil (Exit) eṉṉum iruvāyilkaḷum, uṭaiyatāyiruntatu.
tamiḻ nāṭakamellām icaippaṭṭuḷḷavaiyē (Operas).
nāṭakam eṉpatu nāṭṭakka eṉṟum, araṅku araṅkam eṉpaṉa raṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
ar - araṅku = aṟai, araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭaka mēṭai, nāṭakacālai, viḷaiyāṭiṭam, paṭaikkalam payilumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, āṟṟiṭaik kuṟai, tiruvaraṅkam.
icai nāṭakam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ virivai eṉ muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
(3) maṭainūl:
maṭai = cōṟu, uṇavu. maṭainūl camaiyal nūl.
kantukak karuttum maṭainūṟ ceytiyum 	            (maṇi.2:22)
eṉpatu tamiḻil maṭa nūliruntamaiyait terivikkum.
	kāveri yūṭṭiya kavarkaṇait tūṇip
	pūviri kaccaip pukaḻōṉ taṉmuṉ
	paṉivarai mārpaṉ payanta nuṇporuṭ
	paṉuvaliṉ vaḻāap palvē ṟaṭicil
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai(238-41) kūṟum vīmaṉ maṭainūl, tamiḻ maṭai nūlait taḻuviyatēyeṉpatu ataṉ piṉmaiyālum, avaṉ pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyāṉa tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉāyiruntamaiyālum. aṟiyappaṭum. naḷaṉum tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉē.
(4) maruttuvam.
tamiḻ maruttuvak kalai cittarāl vaḷarkkappeṟṟatu. ataṉāl atu citta maruttuvam eṉappeṟum. kaṭṭikaḷaiyum piḷavaikaḷaiyum karaippatum,oṭinta elumpai oṭṭavaippatum, mutiyavarai iḷaiñarākkuvatum, nūṟṟuk
kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻvippatum, cittamaruttuvam.
	mikiṉum kuṟaiyiṉum nōyceyyum nūlōr
	vaḷimutalā eṇṉiya mūṉṟu. 		           (kuṟaḷ.941)
ātalāl, ūtai (vātam) pittam kōḻai eṉṉum munnāṭiyaiyum nāṭi ataṉāl, iṟṟaik karuvikaḷaik koṇṭu talaiciṟanta tērcci peṟṟa mēlai maruttuvarum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāta,
	nōynāṭi nōymutal nāṭi yatutaṇikkum
	vāynāṭi vāyppac ceyal 		           (kuṟaḷ.648)
citta maruttuvaṉiṉ teyvattiṟamām.
nuḷ - naḷ - nāḷam - nāḷi - nāḻi - nāṭi.
nāṭi pārkkum tiṟamillātāṉ maruntaṉē yaṉṟi maruttuva ṉākāṉ. intiya maruttuvam cittamaruttuvamē. āyurvētam eṉṉum āriya maruttuvam, citta maruttuvattiṉ vaṭanāṭṭuvakaiyē. cēra vēntar iruntavarai citta maruttuvamāyirunta malaiyāḷa maruttuvam, iṉṟu āriya maruttuvamāy māṟiyiruttal kāṇka. orē nōykkup pala maruntukaḷ uḷa. maruntukaḷ iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭum, āyiṉ,maruttuvamuṟai oṉṟē. āriya maruttuvam vērkaḷai mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, citta maruttuvam centūrattai mikutiyākakkoṇṭa teṉṟum, cilar kūṟuvar. avar aṟiyār. "vērpār, taḻaipār, mella mellac centūrac cuṇṇam pār.''  eṉpatu cittamaruttuvap paḻamoḻiyākum. āyiram pēraik koṉṟavaṉ arai maruttuvaṉ. eṉpatu āyiram vēraik koṉṟavaṉ arai maruttuvaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ciṟupañcamūlam, perumpañcamūlam eṉṉum īraintu vērkaḷum, tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē viḷaiyum tamiḻ maruntuc carakkām. maruntu eṉṉum peyarē maṇamuḷḷa vēraiyum taḻaiyaiyum tāṉ kuṟikkum. maru = maṇam. maru - maruntu.
citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟanta maruntu, mūvakai uppuc cērnta muppu eṉpatākum. atu irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟaip poṉṉākkavum ōrilakkam āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻac ceyyavum vallateṉa, ataṉ āṟṟalai uyarvu
naviṟciyākak kūṟuvar.
neṭu nāḷirunta pērum, nilaiyākavēyiṉum kāyakaṟpantēṭi neñcu puṇṇāvar. eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar kūṟiyatu, mūppai nōkkittāṉ pōlum!
aṟuvai (Surgery) muṟaiyum cittamaruttuvattiliruntamai
	mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
	ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
	neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ
	ampucēr uṭampiṉar 			       (patiṟ.42:2-5)
(kayam mūḻkic ciral peyarntaṉṉa neṭaveḷḷūci = kuḷattilē muḻu ciccilik kuruvi eḻukiṉṟakālattu ataṉ vāyalakaippōla, puṇkaḷai nūlāltaikkum pōtu appuṇṇiṉ arattattil maṟainteḻukiṉṟa nīṇṭa veḷḷaiyāṉa ūci. neṭuvaci = nīṇṭa ūcittaḻumpu. vaṭu = kāyppu.)
eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattaṭikaḷālum,
	uṭaliṭait tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉṟai yaṟuttataṉ utira mūṟṟic
	cuṭaluṟac cuṭṭuvēṟōr maruntaṉāl tuyaram tīrvar 									(kamparā.kumpa.146)
	ārār talaivaṇaṅkār ārārtam kaiyeṭār
	ārārtām cattirattil āṟātār - cīrārum
	teṉpuliyūr mēvum civaṉaruḷcēr ampaṭṭat
	tampipukāṉ vācalilē tāṉ.
eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭukkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
maruttuvattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai yaṟintē, paṇṭaik kālattil ūrmaruttuvaṉukku iṟaiyili nilam māṉiyamāka viṭappaṭṭatu. atu
maruttuvappēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. (S.I.1,ii.43).
ulakiṟ ciṟanta cittamaruttuvam, ūkkuvāriṉṟi nāḷukku nāḷ maṟaintum kuṟaintum varukiṉṟatu.
kuḻantai maruttuvam, pēṟukāla maruttuvam, aracamaruttuvam (vilakkamaṟṟatu), nañcu maruttuvam. māṭṭu maruttuvam eṉpaṉa, citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷākum. vilakkam pattiyam.
nīr,karukku (kacāyam) kuḻampu,ney allatu eṇṇey (kirutam) kaḷimpu, meḻuku (lēkiyam), kuḷikai, nīṟu (paspam) mutaliya pala vaṭivilum citta maruntukaḷ uṇṭu. ovvoru nōykkum ovvoru puṟattai uracum māttiraik kaṭṭikaḷum uḷa.

maruttuvaṉukkup paṇṭitaṉ eṉṟum, maruttuvattiṟkup paṇṭitam eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. maruntu koṭuppavaṉ maruttuvaṉ; palporuḷ
kaḷai aṟintavaṉ gṇṭitaṉ.g©oj‹ eṉpatu paṇṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, paṇṭitam eṉpatu paṇṭuvam eṉṟum,maruvum.
paṇṭuvam (Medical Treatment) maruttuvap paṇṭuvam eṉṟum nampikkaip paṇṭuvam (Faith cure) eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭum. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉukkuk kaṭivāyil pāmpuṇik karuṅkal vaippatum, erukkam pūvait tiṉṉak koṭuppatum, mūkkiṟ paccilaiccāṟu piḻivatum, maruttuvap paṇṭuvam; mantirattiṉāl nañcaiyiṟakkuvatu nampikkaip paṇṭuvam.
nampikkaip paṇṭuvam, iṟumpūtu (Miracle), nērttikkaṭaṉ; kuḷicam (Amulet), maṇi, pārvai,mantiram, ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), pāṇippu (pāvakam), muṭṭi, aracak kāṭci mutaliyaṉavākap palavakaippaṭum.
iyēcu perumāṉ toḻu (kuṭṭa) nōyāḷiyait toṭṭu nalappaṭuttiyatum, tiruñāṉa campantar kūṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ curanōyait tīrttatum. iṟampūtu  vaḻipaṭuteyvattainōkki, oṉṟu paṭaippatāka allatu ceyvatāka nērntu koḷvatu nērttikkaṭaṉ, mantira eḻuttuḷḷa takaṭṭaik kaiyiṟ kaṭṭik koḷvatu kuḷicam. uruttirākkam, tuḻāy (tuḷaci) maṇi, muttumālai mutaliya aṇikaḷ maṇiyākum. mantirikaṉ tēṭkoṭṭup paṭṭavaṉai allatu pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭavaṉaip pārttuk kuḻaiyaṭittu mantirippatu pārvai. nañcēṟiyavaṉē mantirattai ōtuvatu mantiram pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭavaṉ kaluḻaṉai (karuṭaṉai) uḷḷuvatu ūḻkam. avaṉ taṉṉaik kaluḻaṉākavē karutuvatu pāṇippu.
tiṭaṅkoḷ mantiram tiyāṉapā vakanilai muṭṭi      
(periya pu.34:1060)
maṇimantiramātiyāl vēṇṭu cittikaḷ  (tāyumā.paripū.9)
cēramāṉ karuvūrēṟiya oḷvāṭ kōpperuñ cēra lirum poṟaiyaik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, nariverūuttalaiyār nalluṭampu peṟṟatāka, 5-ām puṟappāṭṭiṉ koḷukkūṟum, itu, kaṇṭa mālaiyai (Scrofula) aracaṉ toṭiṉ kuṇamākumeṉṟu karuti ataṟku `aracaṉ tīṅku' (King’s evil) eṉṟu āṅkilēyar peyariṭṭa taṉōṭu oppu nōkkattakkatu.
(5)maṇinōṭṭam (irattiṉapparīṭcai)
	kāka pātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum
	ēkaiyum nīṅki iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
	nūlavar noṭitta nuḻainuṇ kōṭi 
	nālvakai varuṇattu nalaṅkēḻ oḷiyavum 
	ēkaiyuma mālaiyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
	pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyavum
	vitimuṟai piḻaiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum
	pūca uruviṉ polanteḷit taṉaiyavum
	tītaṟu katiroḷit teṇmakaṭ ṭuruvavum
	iruḷteḷit taṉaiyavum iruvē ruruvavum
	orumait tōṟṟat taivēṟu vaṉappiṉ
	ilaṅkunīr vīṭūum nalaṅkeḻu maṇikaḷum
	kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum
	tōṟṟiya kuṟṟam tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum
	cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa
	vanta nīrmaiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum
	karuppat tuḷaiyavum kalliṭai muṭaṅkalum
	tirukku nīṅkiya ceṅkoṭi valliyum
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakuti (14:180-198), maṇikaḷiṉ kuṇaṅ kuṟṟaṅ
kaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
6. ōviyam
ōviyac cennūl urainūṟ kiṭakkaiyum
eṉpatu (maṇi 2:32), ōviyanūlait terivikkum.
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyirat toṭarum, cuvarai vaittukkoṇṭaṉṟō cittirameḻuta vēṇṭum? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, māṭaccuvarkaḷilellām akkālattil ōviyam varaiyap peṟṟiruntamaiyai aṟivikkum.
	vampa mākkaḷ kampalai mūtūrc
	cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilai maṉaitoṟum
	maiyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
	evvakai uyirkaḷum uvamam cāṭṭi
	veṇcutai viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
	kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
eṉṟu maṇimēkalai (3:126-131) kūṟutal kāṇka.
oruvaṉ ōr aracaṉatu avaikkaḷam ceṉṟu aṅku aracaṉ taṉ amaiccaruṭaṉamarntiruntataik kaṇṭu,tāṉ koṇṭuvanta kāṇikkaiyai nīṭṭa aracaṉ atai vāṅkāmaiyāl neruṅkic ceṉṟu atu ōr ōviyamāyirukkak kaṇṭāṉeṉṟu, oru katai vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. attakaiya ōviyattiṟavōr akkālattiruntaṉar.
ōviyakkārairaik kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟum (5:30). āṭai aṇikalam kaṭṭiṭam mutaliya ellāp poruḷum ōviya vēlaippaṭuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntaṉa.
(7) uruvam (Sculpture)
maṇṇāl uruvañ ceypavar maṇṇīṭṭāḷar         (maṇi.28:37)
marattālum kallālum poṉvakaiyālum uruvañceypavar
kammiyar; cāntiṉāṟ ceypavar kottar
kammiyanūl tolvaram pellai kaṇṭu       
(tiruviḷai, tirunakaṅ.38)
kōyil tērum kōpuramum uruvaṅkaḷ niṟaintavai. pāvai
yuruvamum pūtappaṭimaiyum pukārilum piṟanakarkaḷilum iruntaṉa.
(8) kaṭṭiṭam
māṭamāḷikaikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum maṇi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, mūvēntar talainakarkaḷilum kōnakarkaḷilum mikuntiruntaṉa.
māḷikai, kōpuram, maṇi, maṇṭapam eṉṉum nāṟcollum teṉcollē.
mālutal māṇpuṟutal.
māṉṟa pūṇmulaiyiṉāḷ(kāñcippu. tirukkaṇ 174). māl perumai. māl - (māḷ) māṇ - māṇpu, māṭci - māḷ - māḷikai -mālikkā (va.) = māṭcimaippaṭṭa maṉai.
kō. = aracu, talaimai. puram = uyarvu, uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam.
purai = uyarcci. puraiuyar pākum (tol.uri.4). vēntaṉ irunta uyarnta eḻunilaik kaṭṭiṭam mutaliṟ kōpuram eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpu ataip pōṟ kōyilil amanta eḻunilai vāṉaḷāvi appeyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ amaippu tērai ottatākum.
kōpuram uḷḷa nakarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē, mutalil puram eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: kāñcipuram, kaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻapuram.
vēntaṉ taṉ talainakarai nāṟpuṟamumnōkkavum, tolai viṟpakaivar varavaik kāṇavum, pakaivar muṟṟukaiyiṭṭu uḻiñaip
pōrai naṭattuṅkatāl noccip pōraik kaṇ kāṇikkavum, avaṉ araṇmaṉaiyiṉ mēl eḻunilaikoṇṭa ōr uyarnta tēr pōṉṟa kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. atu puram, eṉappaṭṭatu. puram = uyarnta kaṭṭiṭamāṉa mēṉmāṭam. puravi = uyarnta cuvarait tāṇṭum kutirai.
puram eṉpatu, piṉpu purattaik koṇṭa araṇmaṉaiyaiyum ataṉ cūḻalaiyum (Acropolis) kuṟittu, ataṉ piṉ, nakar eṉṉum coṟpōl talainakar muḻuvataiyum kuṟittu, nāḷaṭaivil nakarap potup
peyarāyiṟṟu.
araṇmaṉaiyiluḷḷa puram aracaṉ irukkaiyātalāl, kōpuram eṉappaṭṭatu. kō aracaṉ. kō irunta il kōyil eṉappaṭṭatai nōkkuka.
pakaivar varavu kāṇṭaṟkuk kōpuram ciṟanta amaippeṉṟu kaṇṭapiṉ, nakaraic cūḻnta kōṭṭai matililum, vāyiliṟ peritākavum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟiyaṉavākavum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟiyaṉa kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
maturai nakaraic cūḻnta matiliṉ nāṟpuṟattilum, vāyilum māṭamum vāṉaḷāviya kōpuramum iruntaṉa. nāṉku vāyil māṭaṅkaḷ irunta
tiṉāl, maturai nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. kūṭal eṉpatu tamiḻk kaḻakam. atu piṉpu iṭavaṉāku peyarāy maturaiyaik kuṟittatu. itaiyaṟiyātu, tolkataiñar (purāṇikar) oru kataiyaik kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal taṇkōl vēntē 	         (puṟam.58:13)
	tamiḻnilai peṭaṟṟa tāṅkaru marapiṉ
	makiḻnaṉai maṟukiṉ maturai 		    (ciṟupāṇ.66-7)
iṉi, kūṭalnakar eṉpatu nāḷaṭaivil kūṭal eṉak kuṟukiṟṟu eṉiṉumām.
maturainakarvāyil, iṭaiviṭātu oḻukiya vaikaiyāṟu pōl akaṉṟum, iṭaiyaṟāta makkaḷ pōkkuvarattu mikuntum, iruntatu.
	maḻaiyāṭu malaiyiṉ nivanta māṭamoṭu
	vaiyai yaṉṉa vaḻakkuṭai vāyil
eṉṟu maturaikkāñci (355-6) kūṟutal kāṇka.
kōpura maṉṟi vācalai māṭamākavuñ camaittaliṉ, māṭameṉṟār. eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappurai iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
aracaṉukkuriya ciṟappukkaḷellām iṟaivaṉukkum ceyyap peṟṟa
tiṉāl, kōyiltēr mikap peritāyc ceyyappeṟṟatupōl, kōyil matiṟ kōpuramum mikapperiya eḻunilai vāṉaḷāviyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟatu. ataṉ amaippum tērai ottatākum. ataṉ eḻunilaikaḷum tēriṉ eḻutaṭṭukkaḷaip pōṉṟavai. eḻunilai allatu eḻutaṭṭuk karuttu ēḻulakam eṉṉum karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. ēḻulakak karuttum eḻutīvuk karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum.
ēḻuṭaiyāṉ poḻil (tirukkōvai. 7)
taccuk kalaiyil kōyiltērpōl, kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyil kōyiṟ
kōpuram paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ aṟivaiyum āṟṟalaiyum ciṟappakkāṭṭum. tañcaip peruvuṭaiyār kōyiṟ kōpurattiṟku, 10 kal tolaivuluḷḷa cārappaḷḷam eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu cāram kaṭṭiyatākavum, kaṭaikālil oḻukiya nīrttuḷaikaḷai aṭaittaṟkuk kuṟavai mīṉkaḷaip piṭittu viṭṭatākavum, kūṟuvar.
	taṇpuṉal paranta pūcal maṇmaṟuttu
	mīṉīṟ ceṟukkum yāṇarp
	payaṉtikaḻ vaippiṟpiṟar akaṉṟalai nāṭē
eṉpatu (puṟam:7) tañcai nikaḻcciyai oruvāṟu niṉaivuṟuttum.
maṇi = 1. oḷikkal, 2. nīla oḷikkal.
ivviru poruḷkaḷuḷ. mutalāvatu maṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉai
yiṉiṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu maḷkutal eṉṉum viṉaiyi ṉiṉiṉṟum, tōṉṟiyavai yākum. aḻaku eṉpatu ivviraṇṭi ṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya vaḻipporuḷ.
maṇṇutal kaḻuvutal. maṇṇappaṭṭatu maṇṇi. maṇṇi - maṇi.
maṇṇi yaṟipa maṇinalam			         (nāṉmaṇi.5)
maṇṇuṟu maṇiyum 			 	(peruṅ.2-5: 123)
maḷkutal = karuttal, oḷi maḻuṅkutal. maḷku - maṭku - makku.
maḷ - (may) - mai = karumai, karumukil, kariya āṭu.
o.nō: vaḷ(kūrmai) - (vay)-vai = kūrmai
mal - māl = karumai, karumukil, kariya tirumāl.
māl - māri = maḻai. mā - māyōṉ = kariyōṉ.
māl - māri = maḻai, mukil.
maḷ - maḻai = māri, mukil.
maḷ - maṇ - maṇi = kariya(nīla) oḷikkal. karumaiyum nīlamum oṉṟāyk koḷḷap paṭuvatai, kāḷi, nīli; kāḷakaṇṭaṉ, nīlakaṇṭaṉ; kārvaṇṇaṉ, nīlavaṇṇaṉ; karunākam, nīla nākam; mutaliya colliṇai
kaḷāl aṟika.
maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal, mikutal, niṟaital. maṇṭu - maṇṭi = poruḷkaḷ niṟaintu kiṭakkum carakku nilaiyam.
maṇṭu - maṇṭakam = makkaḷ kūṭum maṭam allatu ampalam.
maṇṭakam - maṇṭapam - maṇṭapa(va.)
o.nō: vāṇikam - vāṇipam.
iṉṟum maṇṭakam, maṇṭakappaṭi eṉpatē ulaka vaḻakku.
maṇṭapaṅkaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅ
kaḷum iruntaṉa.
māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mukappil puliyuruvam amaikkappaṭṭum, pulittoṭar eṉṉum caṅkili toṅkaviṭappaṭṭum, iruntaṉa.
pulimuka māṭa malira vēṟi (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa, 9, 69)
pulittoṭar viṭṭa puṉaimāṇ nallil (mullaip.62)
māṭaṅkaḷ maturaiyil mikuntum ciṟantum iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
māṭa maturai (puṟam;32), māṭamali maṟukiṟ kūṭal (muruku.71), māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭal (maturai.429) nāṉ māṭak kūṭal makaḷirum maintarum (kalit.92, matimali puricai māṭak kūṭal (tirumukap paracuram).
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīraik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ celuttutaṟku, karantupaṭai eṉṉum putaicālakam iruntatu. atu terunaṭuviṟ kaṭṭap
paṭṭu yāṉaik kūṭṭam mēṟcellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭap
paṭṭiruntatu. atiṟceṉṟa nīr, yāṉait tutikkai pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
	peruṅkai yāṉai yiṉanirai peyarum
	curuṅkai vīti			                   (cilap. 14:64-5)
kōnakarkaḷil, ūraic cuṟṟik kōṭṭai matil iruntatu. atu puricai eṉappaṭṭatu. purital vaḷaital. puricaiyuḷḷa nakarppeyarkaḷē mutalil puri eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaṉa. kōṭṭai eṉpatum vaḷaitaṟ poruḷatē. kōṭutal vaḷaital.
	uyarvakalam tiṇmai arumai in nāṉkiṉ
	amaivaraṇ eṉṟuraikkum nūl.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkēṟpa , (743) matilaraṇ matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa nālvakaip paṭṭiruntatu.
mika uyaramāṉatu matil; ñāyil eṉṉum ēvaṟaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu eyil; cempai yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyatu iñci; ariya poṟikaḷai yuṭaiyatu cō.
	vāṉuṭkum vaṭinīḷa matil 			               (puṟam.18)
	ey il (ēvaṟaikaḷ) uḷḷatu eyil.
	cempupuṉain tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum puricai
	uvarā vīkait tuvarai 			             (puṟam 201)
cempiṭṭuc ceyta iñcit tirunakarc celvam tēṟi
					              (kamparā. kumpa.160)
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅkai kaṭṭaḻitta
						        (cilap. 17:35)
cō eṉpatu araṇvakaiyē yaṉṟic cōṇitapuram eṉṉum nakarp peyaraṉṟu.
pāmpuri, kottaḷam (Bastion), vāyiṟ kōpuram pataṇam (Rampart), ñāyil mutaliya pala vuṟuppukaḷai yuṭaiyatu. kōṭṭai matil. cila nakarkaḷil ēḻeyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. puṟamatilaic cuṟṟi akaḻi iruntatu.
kōnakarkaḷaip pakaiyaracar muṟṟukaiyiṭṭa kālattil maṟain
tōṭit tappik koḷḷa, neṭuntolaiviṟkuc curaṅkam allatu curuṅkaiyeṉṉum kīḻ nila vaḻikaḷum iruntaṉa.

(9) poṟiviṉai (Machinery)
vēntaṉ taṉ urimaic cuṟṟattōṭu iṉpamāy nīrāṭuvataṟku, vēṇṭumpōtu nīrai nirappavum vaṭikkavum nīrppoṟiyamainta kuḷam iruntatu. atu ilavantikai yeṉappaṭṭatu.
ilavan tikaiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōki (cilap.10:31)
niṟaikkuṟiṉ niṟaittup pōkkuṟiṉ pōkkum
poṟippaṭai yamainta poṅkila vantikai (peruṅ.1:40:311-2)

eṉṉum cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷkaṭku (1:101-4)
poṟkoṭikaḷ patākaiyōṭu koḻikkum tiruntumatil, māṟṟavar maṟappaṭai akaḻaik kaṭantu taṉṉaip paṟṟiṉ, attevvartalai paṉikkumpaṭi nūṟṟuvaraikkolli mutal maranilaiyīṟāka vuḷḷavaiyum, cempurukukaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟai umiḻvaṉavāka yavaṉar tāṭpaṭutta poṟikaḷum, paṉṟi
mutal nuṇṇūlīṟāka vuḷḷavaiyum, tiruntiṟṟeṉka. eṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyar urai varaintuḷḷapaṭi, 103-ām ceyyuḷiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa poṟikaḷaiyē yavaṉar ceytatākat tiruttakka tēvar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avai piṟavaṟṟaip pōl attuṇaic ciṟantaṉa valla vātalālum, tiruttakka tēvarkku ēḻu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muntiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ aṅṅaṉam kūṟāmaiyāṉum, cilappatikāram pōl cintāmaṇi uṇmaik kataiyait taḻuvāmaiyāṉum, tamiḻar kirēkkarukku muṟpaṭṭa iṉat
tārātalālum, mēlai maruttuvattai mēlaiyariṭam kaṟṟa kīḻaiyar mēlai nāṭukaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟuvatu pōl yavaṉar cila poṟikaḷ ceytirukkalāmātalālum, iṟṟai vāṉūrtik koppāṉa mayiṟ poṟiyai yavaṉar ceytatākat tēvar kūṟāmaiyāṉum, cintāmaṇik kūṟṟu koḷḷat takkataṉ ṟeṉṟum, ataṉāl paṇṭait tamiḻap poṟiviṉaik kammiyarkku iḻukkillaiyeṉṟum, kūṟiviṭukka.
(10) poṉnūl
	cāta rūpam kiḷicciṟai āṭakam
	cāmpū natameṉa ōṅkiya koḷkai
eṉpatu (cilap. 14: 201-2), poṉṉiṉ vakaikaḷaik kāṭṭum.
jāta rūppa eṉṉum vaṭacol polinta vaṭivam eṉṟum, 
hāṭṭaka eṉṉum vaṭacol oḷirvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟaip poṉ eṉṟum taṅkam eṉṟum collalām. jampūnata eṉṉum vaṭacol, poṉ (mēru) malaikku vaṭakkil nāvaṟ paḻaccāṟu perukiyōṭuvatāyk karutappaṭṭa āṟṟiṉ peyar. itu āriyat tolkataik koḷkai. itaṉpaṭi, cāmpū natattai nāvalāṟai eṉṟu collal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ ēmāṟuntaṉmaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu, āriyar iṅṅaṉamē ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum tamiḻnāṭṭup poruḷkaṭku vaṭacoṟ peyarkaḷaiyiṭku vaḻakkāṟṟup paṭuttiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉar.
poṉ eṉpatu, poṉpōṉṟa piṟa kaṉiyap poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu , īyam mutaliya piṟa kaṉiyaṅkaḷum paṇṭait tamiḻarkkut terintiruntaṉa. īyam eṉpatu iḷakuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.

(11) itaḷ māṟṟiyam (iracavātam)
cittar itaḷiṉāl (pātaracattāl) tāḻnta kaṉiyaṅkaḷai (ulōkaṅ
kaḷai) veḷḷiyākavum ciṟappākap poṉṉākavum māṟṟiṉatāka, maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. appoṉṉākkam vaṭamoḻiyil (i)raca vātam eṉappaṭum. akkalaiyaik kuṟikkum Alchemy eṉṉum colliliruntu Chemistry eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, kemiya nūlai rasāyaṉam eṉṟaṉar. immuṟaiyil atait tamiḻil itaḷiyam eṉalām.
poṉṉākkam ekipatu (Egypt) nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatāl, akkalai arapiyil alkimiya (al - kimia) eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, al eṉpatu anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cuṭṭuc colleṉṟum, kimiya eṉpatu ekipatu nāṭṭiṉ peyareṉṟum, erutantuṟai āṅkilac curukka akara mutali kūṟum. (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English).
aḷakēcaṉ nikarāka ampoṉ mika vaitta pērum nēcittu racavāta vittaik kalaintiṭuvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar pāṭuvatāl (paripūra.10), itaḷ māṟṟiyak kalai tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
potuvāka, itu cittar kalaiyeṉappaṭum. irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ cittanilaiyaṭaintiruntatiṉāl ikkalaiyai aṟintiruntaṉar.
(12) maṟa nūl
taṉimakkaḷ pōr, paṭaimakkaḷ pōr eṉappōr iru tiṟappaṭum. cilampam, maṟpōr, kuttuc caṇṭai, vāṭpōr mutaliyaṉa taṉi makkaḷ pōrām.
mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūrmallaṉaip porutu koṉṟa kōpperu
naṟkiḷḷi, oru pōravai naṭatti vantāṉ; ataṉāl, pōravaik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ naṭatti vantatu maṟpayiṟcik
kaḷari. maṟpōril, cila uyir nāṭiyāṉa narampukaḷait toṭṭu, etiriyai vīḻttik kollavum, vīḻntavaṉai mūṉṟē mukkāl nāḻikaikkuḷ eḻacceyyavum, cila marumap piṭikaḷ uḷa.
maṟkaḷari, viṟkaḷari, vāṭkaḷari eṉap pōravai allatu muraṇkaḷari palavakaip paṭum.
	iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭai kaṉmār,

	perumarak kampam 				(puṟam. 169 : 9-11)

(13) ōka nūl
ōvutal - ottal, oṉṟutal. ōvu - ōku. ōvu - ōvam -ōkam = aṟivaṉ uḷattil iṟaivaṉōṭu oṉṟum ūḻkam (tiyāṉam).
ukam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil yuka eṉṟum utti eṉpatu pukti eṉṟum āyatupōl, ōkam eṉpatum ammoḻiyil yōka eṉṟākum. immuṟai paṟṟi ōku eṉpatu yōku eṉappaṭum.
ōkap payiṟci eṇṇuṟuppukaḷai yuṭaiyatu. avai oḻukkam (iyamam), oḻuṅku (niyamam), irukkai (ācaṉam), vaḷinilai (pirāṇāyāmam), pulaṉaṭakkam (pirattiyākāram), niṟai (tāraṇai), ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), oṭukkam(camāti) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ irukkaiyum vaḷinilaiyum uṭaṟ
payiṟci; ēṉaiya uḷap payiṟci.
piṟappāl tammai uyarvākak karutum pētaimaiyum cerukku
muḷḷōr, ōkappayiṟci ceyvatu muyaṟkompām. iṭaikalai, piṉkalai, cuḻimuṉai eṉpaṉa vaḷinilait toṭarpāṉa nāṭikaḷaik kuṟikkum teṉ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟai iṭākalā, piṅkalā, sūṣūmuṉā eṉat tirin
tuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
ōka nūliṟ kūṟappaṭum aṟunilaikkaḷaṅkaḷ allatu narappup piṉṉalkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivarai, muṟaiyē, aṇṭi kuṟiyiṭai nālitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum,  aṇṭi koppūḻiṭai āṟitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, koppūḻ maṇṭalattil pattitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, neñcāṅkulai maṇṭalattil paṉṉīritaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, aṭinā maṇṭalattil patiṉāṟitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, iru puruvattiṭai īritaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, iruppatākac collap peṟum.
ōkanūl iṟantu paṭṭatāl aṟunilaikkaḷa (ṣaṭātāra)p peyarkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. irukkaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ palavāṟu tirikkappaṭṭum moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭum uḷa. āsaṉam eṉṉum collē teṉ colliṉ tiripeṉpatu, eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkap peṟum.
(14) māyam (Conjury)
itu mālam allatu kaṇ kaṭṭu.
(15) vaciyam (Enchantment)
itu makaḷiraiyum piṟaraiyum maruntālum mantirattālum maṉap payiṟciyālum vayappaṭuttal.
vayiṉ - vayam - vacam - vaci - vaciyam.
(16) mantirak kaṭṭu
itu tī kāṟṟu mutaliya iyaṟkaip pūtaṅkaḷaiyum, puli nāy pāmpu  mutali uyirikaḷaiyum māntaraiyum, iyaṅkātavāṟum tīṅku ceyyatāta vāṟum mantirattāl taṭuttal.
karaṭi vempuli vāyaiyum kaṭṭalām		     (tāyu.tēcō.8)
(17) makiṭi (mōṭi)
itu mantirattāṟ poruḷkaḷai maṟaittalum avaṟṟai eṭuttalu
mākum.
(18) pēyōṭṭal (Exorcism)
palvakaip pēykaḷaiyum kōṭaṅki allatu uṭukkaṭittu, pēy kōṭpaṭṭāriṉiṉṟu ōṭṭutal pēyōṭṭal ākum.
(19) kuṟaḷi
itu kuṭṭip pēyāl ciṟu kuṟumpukaḷ ceyvittal.
(20) ceyviṉai (Sorcery or Witchcraft)
itu pēyai āḷum mantirakkāraṉaik koṇṭu, vēṇṭātavarkku nōyum cākkāṭum varuvittal.
itu cūṉiyam eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkum.
cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam - cūṉya (va.)
(21) karavaṭa nūl
itu kaḷavu nūl.
	mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam
	tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuviyeṉ
	ṟeṭṭuṭa ṉaṉṟē iḻukkuṭai marapil
	kaṭṭūṇ mākkaḷ tuṇaiyeṉat tirivatu.
eṉpatu (cilap.16:166-9), karavaṭa nūliṉ kūṟukaḷait terivikkum. nimittam tantiram eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku, puḷ viraku eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē nikar teṉcoṟkaḷām.
(22) uṭal nūl
meyp poruḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟēṉum paṭṭāṅku nūl muṟaiyilum, maruttuva muṟaiyilum, pulāluṇavu muṟaiyilum, maṟpōr muṟaiyilum, uṭaṟkūṟukaḷaip paṇṭait tamiḻar naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar.
(23) kāval nūl
	nūlvaḻip piḻaiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ tērcci
	ūrkāp pāḷar.
eṉṉum maturaik kāñciyaṭikaḷaiyum (646-7), avaṟṟiṉ kaḷavu kāṇṭaṟkum kāttaṟkum kūṟiya nūlkaḷ pōvār eṉṉum uraiyaiyum, nōkkuka.
15 aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences)
perumpālum paṭippiṉāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa aṟiviyalkaḷ.
(1) ilakkaṇam
ilakku - ilakkiyam. ilakku - ilakkaṇam. ilakku = kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ. ciṟanta vāḻkkaik kuṟikkōḷāṉa aṟattai eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu ilakkiyam. ciṟanta moḻik kuṟikkōḷāṉa amaippai eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu ilakkaṇam.
	uḷḷuṟai teyvam oḻintatai nilaṉeṉak
	koḷḷum eṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (akat.47)ilakkaṇattaik kuṟiyeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟum, ilakkiyattiṟku aṇaṅkiyam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
ïy¡F - yº(t.)., ilakkiyam - lakṣya (va.) ilakkaṇam -  lakṣṇa - (va.). ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōl, lakṣaṇa lakṣya eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ moḻiyamaitiyaiyum (Grammar) nūṟṟokutiyaiyum (Literature) kuṟippatillai; kuṟi allatu iyalpu, kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷ eṉṉum iraṇṭaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ilakkaṇattaiyum ilakkiyattaiyum kuṟikka, viyākaraṇam (vyākaraṇa) cākittiyam (sāhitya) eṉṉum coṟkaḷaiyē vaṭamoḻiyāḷār āḷkiṉṟaṉar.
ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. 
	oruvaraik kūṟum paṉmaik kiḷaviyum
	oṉṟaṉaik kūṟum paṉmaik kiḷaviyum
	vaḻakki ṉākiya uyarcoṟ kiḷavi
	ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collā ṟalla.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil (510), ilakkaṇam eṉṉum col vantiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ, eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. poruḷil yāppum yāppil aṇiyum aṭaṅkum. paṇṭai yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷil iruntatiṉāl, urainaṭaikkeṉac ciṟappāy ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭavillai. colliṟkē poruḷiruppatāl, colliṟkup  piṉpu poruḷaiyē ilakkaṇap pakutiyāka eṭuttuk
koṇṭaṉar. poruṭpakutiyiṉṟit tamiḻilakkaṇam niṟaivuḷḷatākātu.
tamiḻaic cariyāy uṇarāta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar, poruḷilakkaṇattaip pāṭṭiyal eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar. ilakkiyap poruḷkaṭku ilakkaṇam kūṟuvatē poruḷilakkaṇam eṉa aṟika.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku kālṭuvelār kūṟiyavāṟu vaṭamoḻi yaip piṉpaṟṟiyataṉṟu. vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkē tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiya tākum.
tamiḻ eḻuttu kīṟeḻattum veṭṭeḻuttum eṉa iruvakaip paṭṭatu. muṉṉatu olaiyeḻuttu; piṉṉatu paṭṭaya veḻuttu. paṭṭaya veḻuttaiyē vaṭṭeḻut teṉpar.
iṉṟu acciluḷḷa ōlaiyeḻuttu toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
	tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ
	ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.
eṉṟu 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūl (98) kūṟutal kāṇka. āṇṭu eṉṟatu muṟkālattai. ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟṟatē muṟkāla eḻuttiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉpatu naṉṉūlār karuttu. avar kālattil ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟavillai; peṟṟirukkumāyiṉ atai ēṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum? ātalāl, atu uraiyaṉmai aṟika. akkālam eṉpatu iṉṟum muṟkālattaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil kīṟiyeḻutuvataṟku vaḷai kōṭṭeḻuttum,uḷikoṇṭu paṭṭayattil kuḻitteḻutuvataṟku nērkōṭṭeḻuttumē, ēṟṟatātal kāṇka.
intiya āriyarkku mutalil eḻuttillai. avar maṟai eḻutākkiḷaviyeṉappaṭṭatu. vētakkālap pirāmaṇar tamiḻ nāṭu vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil nūleḻutat tamiḻeḻuttaiyoṭṭik kiranta veḻuttai amaittukkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, ki.pi. 10-ām allatu 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu.
aṟiyappaṭṭa camaṟkiruta mutaṟpaḻaṅkalveṭṭu, kattiyavāril cuṉākar eṉṉumiṭattil oru pāṟai mēl uḷatu. atu uruttira tāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉa vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālam ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. atu nākariyil illai; paḻaiya kalveṭṭeḻuttil uḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuriya pavar kaiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ, nākariyai nōkkiya mikunta muṉṉēṟṟattaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; atē camaiyattil tantiturukkaṉiṉ ki.pi.750-ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu, iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyaip peritum otta kuṟikaḷiṉ muḻut tokutiyaiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmaiyāṉa iṟṟai nākariyuluḷḷa mutaṟkalveṭṭu, ki.pi.11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muntiyataṉ ṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (māṉiyar ulliyamcu camaṟkiruta- āṅkila akara mutali - muṉṉurai, g¡.XXVIII,  aṭik kuṟippu).
tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttaip piṉpaṟṟiyatē eṉpatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyilumillai.
tamiḻar iṉṉicaik kalaiyilum nāṭakakkalaiyilum ciṟantirunta
tiṉāl, moḻiyoṭu avviru kalaikaḷaiyum cērttu, tamiḻai iyalicai nāṭakameṉa muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṉar. ittakaiya moḻiyamaippiṭam vēṟeṅkaṇumillai.
tamiḻar iyalumiṭamellām tam viṉaikaḷai icaiyoṭu ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu, tālāṭṭup pāṭṭu, ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭu, naṭa vaippāṭṭu, mukavaippāṭṭu, ēṟṟap pāṭṭu, ēlap pāṭṭu. vaḷḷaippāṭṭu, kaḻiyaṟ pāṭṭu, kummip pāṭṭu, kōlāṭṭappāṭṭu. ūñcaṟ pāṭṭu, vaḻinaṭaic cintu , oppu (oppāri)p pāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷilum irunta ilakkaṇamellām mut tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷē. iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇam piṇṭam eṉṟum, muttamiḻilakkaṇam māpiṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum.
piṇṭittal = piṭittal, tiraṭṭutal piṇṭi - piṇṭam = tiraḷai. piṇṭi - piṭi. o.nō: taṇṭi - taṭi. piṭittal = tiraṭṭutal, kaikkuḷ tiraḷak koḷḷutal. pori viḷaṅkāy piṭittal eṉṉum vaḻakkaiyum, piḷḷaiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu, eṉṉum paḻamoḻi  yaiyum, nōkkuka.
poruḷilakkaṇam poruḷkaḷai akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar kātaliṉ pattai akam eṉac ciṟappittu, maṟṟellāvaṟṟaiyum puṟattuḷ aṭakkum. puṟattuḷ pōrttuṟai mikac ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, maṟṟavaiyellām eḻupuṟap poruḷ tiṇai
kaḷuḷ āṟāvatāṉa vākait tiṇaiyuḷ aṭakkappeṟum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivai eṉ tolkāppiya viḷakkam eṉṉum nūluṭ kaṇṭu koḷka. ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻ mikunta ilakkaṇa varampuḷḷatu.
	kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
	paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
	maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
	eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar (tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam) kūṟutal kāṇka.
(2) moḻi nūl
moḻi nūṟku vittūṉṟiyavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻc coṟkaḷai, ilakkaṇa vakaic col mūṉṟum poruḷvakaic col oṉṟum coṟpiṟappiyal vakaic col mūṉṟumāka vakuttiruntaṉar.
	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.  	    (tol.peyar.1)
	colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaieṉṟu
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē. 	    (tol.peyar 4)
	iṭaic col kiḷaviyum uriccol kiḷaviyum
	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkil tōṉṟum eṉpa. 	    (tol.peyar 5)
iyaṟcol tiricol ticaiccol eṉa mūṉṟiyalum ceyyuḷ īṭṭaccollē.
	centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
	taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi. 	      (tol.ecca.4)
	annāṟ collum toṭukkuṅ kālai
	valikkumvaḻi valittalum melikkumvaḻi melittalum
	virikkumvaḻi virittalum tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum
	nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum kuṟukkumvaḻik kuṟukkalum
	nāṭṭal vaḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar. 		       (tol.ecca 7)
innūṟpākkaḷum, mutaṉilai, iṭainilai, īṟu, uṟupu, puṇarcci, cāriyai mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷum, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ moḻi nūlaṟivaikkāṭṭum.
iyaṟcol eṉpatu vērc collum aṭic collumāṉa iyalpāṉa col (Primitive mood). tirinta col eṉpatu atiṉiṉṟu tirinta col (Derivative mood). ivai yiraṇṭum centamiḻc col. ticaiccol eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc col. akkālattil vaṭa collum piṟa ayaṟcollum tamiḻiṟ kalakkavillai.
(2)aṟanūl
itu poruḷkaḷai aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttu, nalloḻukkattaip paṟṟik kūṟum.
(3) poruḷ nūl
itu ellārum tattam toḻilāṟ poruḷīṭṭuvataṟku, iṉṟiyamaiyāta pātukāppuc ceyyum araciyalaip paṟṟikkūṟum.
(4) iṉpa nūl
itu āṭavar peṇṭir kāmaviṉpattaic ciṟappittuk kūṟum. iṉpa vāḻkkaiyaik kūṟuvatu akapporuḷ nūl eṉṟum iṉpat tuyppai maṭṭum kūṟuvatu kāmanūl allatu vēṇūl eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
(5)maṟainūl (Theology)
itu perumpālum camayak kuṭumikaḷai allatu koṇmuṭipu kaḷaik kūṟum. itu mantiram eṉavum vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. maṉṉum tiram (tiṟam) mantiram. maṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal. itu uṇmai
yākum eṉṟu tiṇmaiyāy eṇṇic colvatu mantiram.
pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymori piciyē 		   (tol.cey.78)
	niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
	maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa. 		(tol.cey.176)
muṉṉutal karututal. muṉṉam = maṉam. muṉ - maṉ - maṉam. iṉi, muṉṉam- muṉam - maṉam eṉṟumām.
mantiram camayak koḷkai paṟṟiyatum cāvippu vāḻttup paṟṟi yatum eṉa iru vakaippaṭum.
camayak koḷkai paṟṟiyatum, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, koṇmuṭipu nūl eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. nammāḻvār aruḷic ceyta tiruvāymoḻi kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu; tirumūlar aruḷic ceyta tirumantiram koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) nūl.
	niṟaimoḻi māntar perumai nilattu
	maṟaimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum.
eṉṟatu (kuṟaḷ.28) ittakaiya nūlkaḷai nōkkiyē.
cāvippu vāḻttu moḻikaḷai mantiram eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku; nūl vaḻakkaṉṟu.
6.paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy)
paṭṭāṅku uṇmai. paṭṭāṅku nūl meypporuḷ nūl. māntaṉ uṭalamaippup paṟṟit tamiḻar kaṇṭa meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ), 96. avai ātaṉ (āṉma) meyporuḷ 24 , nāṭi10, nilai (avattai) 5, malam 3,kuṇam 3, maṇṭalam 3, piṇi 3. tiripu (vikāram) 8, nilaikkaḷam (ātāram)  6, tātu 7 ūtai (vāyu) 10. uṟai (kōcam) 5, vāyil 9, eṉpaṉa.
ātaṉ meyp poruḷ 24 āvaṉa: - pūtam 5, pulaṉ 5, aṟivup pulaṉ 5, karumappulaṉ 5, karaṇam 4.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum aimpūtamāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; āṇṭavaṉ (pati), ātaṉ (pacu), ācu(pācam) eṉa mūṉṟāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; uyir, mey (uṭampu) eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; tamiḻariṉ vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkaikaḷām.
ātaṉukku iṟaivaṉōṭuḷḷa toṭarpumuṟai irumai (tuvaitam), oṉṟiya irumai (viciṣṭāttu vaitam) eṉa iruvakaiyākavē, paṇṭait tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. orumai (attuvaitam) tamiḻar koḷkai
yaṉṟu.
tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl muṉivaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟatiṉāl, uyir, mey, uyirmey, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam, puṇarcci, mutaliya eḻuttilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; peyar, viṉai mutal, eṇ vēṟṟumai, viṉai, viṉaimuṟṟu. viṉaiyeccam, iṟantakāla viṉai, nikaḻkāla viṉai, etirkāla viṉai mutaliya collilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; meypporuḷ nūṟkaruttum taḻuvumāṟu amaikkap
peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattai eṉ centamiḻc ciṟappu. muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇka.
(7) aḷavai nūl
itu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟitaṟku ētāṉa (ētuvāṉa) aḷavaikaḷai (piramāṇaṅkaḷai) eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu. aḷavaikaḷ mottam pattu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉavai kāṭci karuttu oppu urai eṉṉum nāṉku. karutteṉpatu uyttuṇarvu.
(8) ēraṇam (Logic)
itu aḷavaikaḷaik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟiyum vakaikaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu.
vaṭivēl ceṭṭiyār patippitta tarkka paripāṣaieṉṉum nūliṟutiyil, akattiyar pērāl 20 tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu,
	poruḷkuṇam karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumai
	iṉmai yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu. itu poruḷkaḷai ēḻākap pakuppatu. itaiyē piṟkālattil vaṭavar vaicēṭikam eṉṟaṉar.
ēraṇam uravam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam   (taṉippāṭal)
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar       (tirukkōvaic ciṟappuppāyiram).
(9) vāṉa nūl (Astronomy)
vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ (naṭcattiram), kōḷ (kirakam) , ōrai (irāci) mutaliyavaṟṟai viḷakkik kūṟuvatu vāṉa nūl. irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum eḻu kōḷkaḷum paṉṉīrōraikaḷum kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar.
eḻu kōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl ēḻu nāṭkiḻamaiyai mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭuttiyavar tamiḻarē. atu piṉṉar ulaka muḻutum paraviyuḷḷatu.
koḷ - kōḷ. koḷḷutal vaḷaital. koḷ - koṭku. koṭkutal = cuḻalutal, cuṟṟi varutal. koṭpatu kōḷ. kōḷkaḷ vāṉattiṟ cuḻaṉṟu cuṟṟivarum tōṟṟattāl appeyar peṟṟaṉa. irāku kētu eṉpaṉa cāyaikaḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. niṟam paṟṟi avai aṇivakaiyil karumpāmpu, cempāmpu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iruttal karuttal. ir - irā - irāku. cē - cētu = civappu. cētāmpal cevvām pal. cētu - kētu. o.nō: cempu - kempu.
tamiḻ 		vaṭamoḻi 		āṅkilam
ñāyiṟu 		ātittaṉ 		Sun-Sunday
tiṅkaḷ 		cōmaṉ 			Moon-Monday
cevvāy 		(maṅkaḷam) 		Mars-Tuesday
aṟivaṉ 		putaṉ 			Mercury-Wednesday
viyāḻaṉ 		pirukaspati(kuru)		Jupiter-Thusday
veḷḷi  		cukkiraṉ 			Venus-Friday
kāri    		(caṉi) 			Saturn-Saterday
vaṭamoḻikkiḻamaip peyarkaḷuḷ, maṅkaḷa vāram, caṉi vāram ākiya iraṇṭu tavira maṟṟavaiyellām nēr moḻipeyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
āṅkilap peyarkaḷuḷ, piṉ aintum piṟkālattil māṟi viṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, eḻunāḷ eṉṉum kāla aḷavu māṟātiruttal kāṇka.
cevvāy cenniṟamuḷḷateṉṟum, viyāḻaṉ poṉṉiṟamuḷḷa teṉṟum, veḷḷi veṇmaiyāṉateṉṟum, kāri kariyateṉṟum, kaṇṭaṟintatu viyakkat takka ceytiyām.
viyal = 1. perumai.
mūḻttiṟutta viyaṉṟāṉai 			          (patiṟ.33,5).
		2. akalam
viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē.           (tol.col 354).
		3.mikuti. (cilap.3,7, urai).
viyal - viyalaṉ - viyāḻaṉ.
viḷḷutal = virital, malartal. viḷ - viy - viyal.
pirukaspati eṉṉum vaṭacol viyāḻaṉ eṉpataṉ moḻi peyarppāyiruppatōṭu, peruku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripai nilaimoḻi mutaṉilaiyākak koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
paṉiṉiru oraikaḷ
tamiḻ 	vaṭamoḻi 	ilattīṉ
mēḻam 	mēṣam 	Aries
viṭai 	riṣapam 	Taurus
iraṭṭai 	mituṉam	Gemini
alavaṉ 	karkkaṭakam	Cancer
āḷi 	cimmam 	Leo
kaṉṉi 	kaṉṉi 	Virgo
tulai 	tulā 	Libra
naḷi 	viruccikam 	Scorpion
cilai 	cāpam 	Sagitarius
cuṟavam 	makaram 	(Capricornus)
kumpam 	kumpam 	Aquarius
mīṉam 	mīṉam 	Pisces
vaṭamoḻiyil ellāp peyarkaḷum, ilattīṉil patiṉoru peyarkaḷum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ nēr moḻipeyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
muḻuttal tiruḷutal. muḻutta āṇ piḷḷai eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. muḻutu mottam. muḻu - muḻā = tiraṇṭa muracu. muḻā - muḻavu - muḻavam. muḻā - miḻā = tiraṇṭa māṉ (Stag). miḻā - mēḻam = tiraṇṭa cemmaṟiyāṭṭukkaṭā. mēḻam - mēṣa(va.) mēḻam - mēḻakam - ēḻakam - ēṭakam - ēṭu - yāṭu - āṭu.
viṭaiyum kaṉṉiyum muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
tullutal = poruntutal, ottal. tul - tuṉ. tuṉṉutal poruntutal. tul - tulai = oppu. iru puṟamum otta niṟaikōl. tul - tulā = tulai pōṉṟa ēṟṟam. tulā - tulām - tulāṉ = oru niṟai. o.nō: oppu = tulai. opparāvi = tulai ceyvōṉ. kummutal kuvital. kam - kampu - kumpam - kumpa (va.). miṉ - mīṉam -mīṉa (va.).
paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil paṉṉī rōraip peyarkaḷē paṉṉiru mātap peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
mati - mātam - māsa(va.). ovvoru piṟai nilaiyum pakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. vaḷarpiṟai veṇpakkam eṉṟum, tēypiṟai karumpakkam eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
paku - pakkam. paku - Bhaj (va.). pakkam-pakṣa(va.).
irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟalai, mūtirai, kaḻai, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇai , uttaram, kai, aṟuvai, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉai, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭaikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākkai, cekku, nāḻi , muracu, tōṇi eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu. ivaiyallāta potu uṭukkaḷ veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa. pukaikkōḷ (Comet) vāl
veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
oru pakalum ōr iravum cērntatu oru nāḷ eṉṟum ēḻu nāḷ koṇṭatu oru kiḻamaiyeṉṟum, oru vaḷarpiṟaiyum, oru tēypiṟaiyum cērntatu oru mātam eṉṟum, katiravaṉ paṉṉīrōraik kuḷḷum pukuvatu paṉṉiru mātam eṉṟum, ataṉ oru vaṭa celavum oru teṉ celavum cērntu ōr āṇṭeṉṟum, kaṇakkiṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. oru nāḷai āṟu ciṟu poḻutākap pirittatu pōṉṟu, ōr āṇṭai āṟu perum poḻutākap pirittiruntaṉar.
oru kōnakart tōṟṟam allatu oru pēraracaṉ piṟappup pōṉṟa nikaḻcciyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, toṭarāṇṭu kaṇittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
	ceññā yiṟṟuc celavum añ ñāyiṟṟup
	parippum parippuc cūḻntamaṇ ṭilamum
	vaḷitiri taru ticaiyum
	vaṟitu nilaiiya kāyamum eṉṟivai
	ceṉṟaṉan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
	iṉaitteṉ pōrum uḷarē
eṉṉum (puṟam.30) uṟaiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār kūṟṟum, 
mākavicumpu (puṟam.35, akam. 253, maturaikkā.454, pari.1) eṉṉum  vaḻakkum, mākamāvatu pūmikkum cuvarkkattukkum naṭuvu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar uraiyum,
	iṉṉicai yeḻiliyai irappavum iyaivatō
	vaḷitarum celvaṉai vāḻttavum iyaivatō?
eṉṉum pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkōviṉ pāṭṭaṭikaḷum (kalit.15), kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
maḻaikkum kāṟṟiṟkum katiravaṉ karaṇiyam eṉpatai muṉṉait tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar. eḻili mukil. eḻiliyait tōṟṟavikkum katiravaṉai eḻili eṉṟatu ilakkaṇaiyeṉṉum ākupeyarp pōli.
(10) kaṇiyam (Astrology)
oruvar piṟanta nāḷaiyum vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷkōḷ nilai yaiyum aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittu, avarukku, varum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷai muṟpaṭak kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu kaṇiyam. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvataṟkum varaiyappaṭum kaṇiyamē piṟappiyam (jātakam).
kōccēramāṉ yāṉaikkaṭ cēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟai iṉṉanāḷ iṟappāṉ eṉṟu, kūṭalūrkiḻār oru viṇvīḻ koḷḷiyaik kaṇṭu kaṇittaṟinta ceytiyai 229-ām puṟappāṭṭuk kūṟum. nalla nāḷum vēḷaiyum pārttu viṉaikaḷait toṭaṅkuvatu, paṇṭaināḷiṟ peruvaḻakkamā yiruntatu. nāḷē raṭittal, kuṭaināṭkōḷ, vāḷnāṭ
kōḷ eṉpaṉa itait terivikkum.
toṉṟu toṭṭuk kaṇiyat toḻil ceytu varum tamiḻ vakuppāṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ. vaḷ kūrmai. vaḷḷuvaṉ kūrmatiyaṉ. āriyak kulap pirivāl, avaṉ tāḻttappaṭṭup pēriṭaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum piḻaip piḻantāṉ.
kaṇṇutal = akakkaṇṇāṟ pārttal, karututal, matittal. kaṇṇiyam matippu. kaṇ - kaṇi. kaṇittal = matittal, kaṇakkiṭutal, aḷaviṭutal. kaṇi - kaṇitam - kaṇicam = matippu (Aprroximation). kaṇi - kaṇikai = tāḷam kaṇittāṭupavaḷ. kaṇi - kaṇikaṉ. kaṇiyaṉ = nāḷ kōḷ nilaikaṇṭu varuṅkāla naṉmai tīmai kaṇippavaṉ. kaṇ, kaṇi, kaṇitam, kaṇikai eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil  mutaleḻut teṭup
pōcaiyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇitam eṉpatu kaṇita eṉṟum, kaṇikai eṉpatu kaṇikā eṉṟum, īṟu kuṉṟiyum tirintum vaḻaṅkum. 
(11) kaṇakku
kaḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal, kūṭutal, kaḷḷa =
okka (uvama urupu), kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭumiṭam. kaḷ - kaḷa - kaṇa. kaṇattal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal. kaṇakka = pōla. kuraṅku kaṇakka ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. kaṇa - kaṇam = kūṭṭam. kaṇavaṉ kūṭukiṉṟavaṉ. kaṇa - kaṇakku = kūṭiya tokai, aḷavu
kaṇakku eṉpatu mutalil kūṭṭal kaṇakkai maṭṭum kuṟittu, piṉpu nālvakaik kaṇakkiṟkum potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
paṇṭait tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērntavar eṉpatu, avar kaiyāṇṭa nuṇṇiya aḷavaikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.
eṇṇalaḷavai
kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakka vāypāṭu

akkālattil araciyalārāl nilam evvaḷavu nuṭpamāy aḷakkappaṭṭateṉpatu,
iṟaiyili nīṅkunilam mukkālē iraṇṭu mākāṇi araikkāṇi muntirikaik kīḻ araiyē iraṇṭumā mukkāṇik kīḻ mukkālē nāṉkumā araikkāṇi muntirikaik kīḻ nāṉkumāviṉāl iṟaikaṭṭiṉa kāṇik kaṭaṉ  eṉpatāl viḷaṅkum.
iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa aḷavu 1/52000 ākum.
mēlvāyilakkap pēreṇkaḷ
tolkāppiyattil, ilakkam nūṟāyiram eṉṉum toṭarc collāl kuṟikkap paṭṭālum, ilakkam eṉṉum colliṉmaiyāl, āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pērilakkap peyarkaḷ tamiḻil illaiyeṉap palar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyarē,
	aiam paleṉa varūum iṟuti
	alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal nilaiyum.
eṉap (393) pala kōṭikaḷaik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷaik kuṟittalāl, ilakkam, kōṭi eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ avar kālattil tamiḻil illaiyeṉpatu poruntātu.
	aṭukkiya kōṭi peṟiṉum kuṭippiṟantār
	kuṉṟuva ceytal ilar.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷil (954), kōṭi vantiruttal kāṇka. aṭukkiya kōṭikaḷaik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷiṟ cila varumāṟu:
kumpam = āyiraṅ kōṭi
kaṇikam = pattāyiraṅ kōṭi
tāmarai = kōṭā kōṭi
caṅkam = pattuk kōṭā kōṭi
vāraṇam = nūṟu kōṭā kōṭi
paratam = ilakkam kōṭik kōṭā kōṭi.
(1iṉ piṉ 24 cuṉṉaṅ koṇṭatu.)
ai am paleṉa varuum iṟuti eṉṟu tolkāppiyar (tol.393) kuṟippāy īṟupaṟṟiccoṉṉavaṟṟai:
	neytalum kuvaḷaiyum āmpalum caṅkamum
	maiyil kamalamum veḷḷamum
eṉṟu (paripāṭal,2;13-14) kīrantaiyār veḷippaṭaiyāyk kūṟiṉār. kamalam (va.) tāmarai.
tāmarai, kuvaḷai eṉpaṉa aiyīṟu; kaṇikam, caṅkam, veḷḷam eṉpaṉa ammīṟu; āmpal pallīṟu; neytal allīṟu. itait tolkāppiyar kuṟittilar. āmpal kumutam eṉṟum kuṟikkappeṟum.
kumpam, tāmarai, caṅkam, vāraṇam eṉpaṉa tūya teṉ coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattai eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka. caṅkam caṅku. tāmarai eṉṉum eṇṇaip patumam eṉṟu moḻipeyarttuk kūṟuvar vaṭavar; muḷari eṉṟu oru poruḷ maṟucollāṟ kuṟippar kampar.
tāmarai - tāmaraca(va.). caṅku - caṅkam - Sankha (va.)
kaṇakku allatu kaṇita nūl eṇṇūl eṉavum peyar peṟum. ērampam eṉṉum paṇṭait tamiḻk kaṇita nūl iṟantupaṭṭatu.
ciṟṟilakkattiṟkum pērilakkattiṟkum perukkal vāypaṭiruntatu pōṉṟē, catura (Square) vāyppāṭum paṇṭaik kālattiliruntatu. atu kuḻikkaṇakku eṉappeṟṟatu. kuḻittal caturittal. atu ōr eṇṇai avveṇṇaikkoṇṭē perukkutal. ciṟṟilakkak kuḻippu ciṟu kuḻiyeṉṟum, pērilakkak kuḻippu peruṅkuḻiyeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kuḻi - kuṇi - gun (va.).
ḻa - ṇa. o. nō: āḻi - āṇi - āḻamāyiṟaṅkuvatu. āṇivēr āḻamāy iṟaṅkum vēr.
taḻal - taṇal.
kuṇittal perukkutal, caturittal.
kuṇi + aṉam = kuṇaṉam.
(12) uṭaṟkuṟi nūl (Physiognomy)
talai, kaḻuttu, mārpu, kaikāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiluḷḷa vari (irēkai), maṟu, maccam, cuḻi mutaliyavaṟṟaikkoṇṭum; av vuṟuppuk
kaḷiṉ vaṭivu, niṟam mutaliyavaṟṟaikkoṇṭum; makkaḷiṉ iyalpu
kaḷaiyum avarkku nērum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu uṭaṟ kuṟi nūlākum.
kaivari nūl (Palmistry) oru taṉi nūlāy vaḻaṅki variṉum, atu uṭaṟkuṟi nūliṉ oru pirivē.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟṟaik kaṇṭu avarukku aracāḷum tiruppoṟi yuṇṭeṉṟu kuṟikāraṉ kūṟiyatu, uṭaṟkuṟi nūl taḻuviyē.
	nuntai tāḷniḻal iruntōy niṉṉai
	araicuvīṟ ṟirukkum tiruppoṟi yuṇṭeṉṟu
	uraicey tavaṉ 			            (cilap.30: 174-6)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pattiṉit teyvak kūṟṟāyk kūṟutal kāṇka.

(13) puḷ nūl
vallūṟu, āntai, kākam, karikkuruvi, kāṭai mutaliya paṟavai
kaḷiṉ kuralaiyum iyakkattaiyum koṇṭu, varappōkum naṉmai tīmaikaḷaik kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu puḷ nūl.
puḷ paṟavai. puḷḷāl aṟiyappaṭum kuṟiyaip puḷ eṉpatu ākupeyar.
vaḻiccelvār vāyiṉiṉṟu taṟceyalāy varum collaik kuṟiyākak koḷvatu, vāyppuḷ eṉappaṭum.
uṭampiṉ palavuṟuppukkaḷ tuṭippataik kūṟum tuṭinūlum, kaṭṭu eṉṉum neṟkuṟiyum, kaḻaṅku eṉṉum kāykkuṟiyum , ētēṉum ōr ēṭṭait toṭum toṭukuṟiyum, eṇkuṟiyum, peyarkkuṟiyum, niṉṟa nilaik kuṟiyum kavaṭikkuṟiyum, kaṇṭa kāṭcik kuṟiyum nērnta nikaḻccik kuṟiyum, coṉṉa
coṟ kuṟiyum, kōṭiḻaittaṟkuṟiyum, mūccu viṭaṟkuṟiyum. tēvarāḷar
kuṟiyum, viriccikkuṟiyum, (Oracle) iraṇṭiloṉṟaṉ kuṟiyum, palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷāṉa naṟkuṟi tīkkuṟikaḷum piṟavum puḷnūliṉpāṟpaṭuvaṉavē.
(14) kaṉā nūl
iṉṉa yāmattil iṉṉa poruḷ allatu nikaḻcci kāṇiṉ, ivvaḷavu kālattil iṉṉatu nērum eṉṟu kūṟum nūl kaṉā nūlām.
maturait tamiḻc caṅkam veḷiyiṭṭa oru kaṉā nūl iṉṟum uḷatu.
(15) uḷa nūl (Psychology)
taṉippaṭṭavarum tokutiyāḷarumāṉa makkaḷiṉ uḷappāṅku kaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu uḷa nūl.
itu tolkāppiya meyppāṭṭiyalāl aṟiyappaṭum. eṇcuvai
kaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum, mutaṉ mutal eṭuttuk kūṟiyatu tamiḻilakkaṇamē.
(16) pūta nūl
nilam nīr tī vaḷi veḷi eṉṉum aimpūtaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷaik kūṟum nūl pūta nūl.
``	karuvaḷar vāṉat ticaiyiṉ tōṉṟi
	uruvaṟi vārā oṉṟaṉ ūḻiyum
	untuvaḷi kiḷarnta ūḻuḻ ūḻiyum
	centīc cuṭariya ūḻiyum paṉiyoṭu
	taṇpeyal talaiiya ūḻiyum avaiyiṟṟu
	uṇmuṟai veḷḷam mūḻki yārtarupu
	mīṇṭum pīṭuyar pīṇṭi avaṟṟiṟkum
	uḷḷī ṭākiya irunilat tūḻiyum''		        (pari. 2 : 5-12)
``	kaltōṉṟi maṇtōṉṟāk kālattu''	           (pu.ve. 35)
``	malaimāṟiya viyaṉñālattu	                           (maturai. 4)
``	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭaintum
	kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ''		     (tol. mara. 90)
``	vañca maṉattāṉ paṭiṟṟoḻukkam pūtaṅkaḷ
	aintum akattē nakum.''			          (kuṟaḷ. 271)
ivai aimpūtaṅkaḷaip paṟṟip paṇṭaiyat tamiḻarkkirunta aṟivaip pulappaṭuttum.
(17) nila nūl
nilattiṉ vakaikaḷaiyum nilappaṭai vakaikaḷaiyum maṇṇiṉ vakaikaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu nila nūl.
(18) nīr nūl
kiṇaṟu tōṉṟuvataṟku nīrirukkum iṭaṅkaḷait terivippatu nīr nūl. itu kūva nūl eṉavumpaṭum.
(19) putaiyal nūl
putaiyal irukkumiṭaṅkaḷai aṟiyac ceyvatu putaiyal nūl.
(20) kōḻi nūl
itu pōrc cēvaṟ kōḻikaḷiṉ niṟaṅkaḷaiyum tiṟaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu.
(21) pari nūl
itu palvakaik kutiraikaḷaiyum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaiyellām viḷakkik kūṟuvatu.
(22) yāṉai nūl
itu yāṉaiyaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaiyellām virivākak kūṟuvatu.
(23) varalāṟṟu nūl
mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟil pāṇṭiyar tokai kuṟikkappaṭṭiruntālum, aṉṟāṭa naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaip paṭṭōlaip perumāṉ eḻutivantatālum, paraṇi eṉṉum vākaip paṉuvalkaḷil araca vaḻimarapu kūṟappaṭuvatālum, varalāṟṟu nūl oruvakaiyil eḻutappaṭṭu vantamai uyttuṇarappaṭum. mūvēntar kuṭi varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷum avar varalāṟṟuk karuvi nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
``	ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam
	tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampamnīr nilamu lōkam
	māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
	vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻi vaḻip peyarum māḷa.''
eṉpatu, iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻkkalai nūl vakaikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟai eṭuttuk kūṭum taṉippāviṉam. piṟkālac ceyyuḷātalāl, cila kalai nūlkaḷ vaṭacoṟ peyarāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(25) tiṇai nūl
mutal karuuri eṉṉum mūvakaip poruḷaiyum paṟṟik kūṟum tiṇai nūl, oru vakaiyil ñālanūlai (Geography) okkum.
(26) poḻutu pōkku
paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ oḻivu nērattaiyum ōyvu nāṭkaḷaiyum piṉvarumāṟu kaḻittu vantaṉar.
ciṟuvar:
kōliyaṭittal, tel teṟittal, kiḷittaṭṭu, caṭukuṭu, killit
tāṇṭu, pāṇṭi (cillākku), mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭāṭal, kāṟṟāṭi viṭutal, maramēṟutal, nīruḷ mūḻki viḷaiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
iḷaiñar:
caṭukuṭu, kiḷittaṭṭu mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭāṭal, maṟpayiṟci iḷavaṭṭakkal tūkkal, kariyalaṭittal, vēṭṭaiyāṭal, ēṟukōḷ (callikkaṭṭu) mutaliyaṉa.
mutiyōr.
cēvaṟpōr, kaṭāppōr, katuvālippōr, kāṭaippōr, puṟāppōṭṭi mutaliyaṉa kāṇal, vēṭṭaiyāṭal,tūṇṭil pōṭutal, paṇaiyam vaittum vaiyātum tāyamāṭutal mutaliyaṉa.
ciṟumiyar:
vīṭukaṭṭi viḷaiyāṭal, kummiyaṭittal, pūppaṟittal, kaḻaṅ kāṭal, teḷḷēṇam koṭṭal, ammāṉaiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
peṇṭir:
kiḷi vaḷarttal, pūvai (nākaṇam) vaḷarttal, puṟā vaḷarttal, māṉ vaḷarttal, mālai toṭuttal, tāyamāṭutal, kuravai yāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
aracar:
kutiraip pantayam, tērppantayam, yāṉaippōr, cākkaik kūttu mutaliyaṉa kāṇal;  yāḻppōr, icaippōr, āṭaṟpōr, ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi, paṭṭimaṉṟam, palkavaṉa araṅku, nūlaraṅkēṟṟam mutaliya naṭappittal; vēṭṭaiyāṭal, paṉaiyam vaittut tāyamāṭal, urimaic cuṟṟattuṭaṉ ilavantikaic cōlaiyil viḷaiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
ūrār:
tiruviḻāk koṇṭāṭal, nāṭakam naṭippittal, ēṟukōḷ
naṭattutal, mutaliyaṉa. ēṟukōḷ iṉṟu callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum mañcu veruṭṭu eṉṟum māṭupiṭi caṇṭai eṉṟum collappeṟum.
nakarattār:
vēntaṉ (intira) viḻāk koṇṭāṭal, putuppuṉalāṭal mutaliyaṉa. vēntaṉ viḻāvaik koṇṭāṭumpōtu piṟa teyvaṅkaṭkum pūcaikaḷ naṭattiṉar.
2
paṇṭait tamiḻap paṇpāṭu
1. potu
 mēlai nāṭukaḷellām nākarikamaṭaintiruntālum, avaṟṟuḷ āṅkila nāṭē paṇpāṭṭiṟ ciṟantatākac collappaṭuvatupōl, nāvalan
tēyattilum tamiḻ nāṭē nallatākac collappaṭṭatu.
	vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
	āyiṭait tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu
eṉṟu, tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyiram iyaṟṟiya paṉampāraṉār kūṟutal kāṇka.
	vaṭukar aruvāḷar vāṉkaru nāṭar
	cuṭukāṭu pēyerumai eṉṟivai yāṟum
	kuṟukār aṟivuṭaiyār.
eṉpatu oru paḻañ ceyyuḷ (tol. col 55. cēṉā. urai mēṟkōḷ).
	ciṟappuṭai marapiṟ poruḷum iṉpamum
	aṟattuvaḻip paṭūum tōṟṟam pōla
eṉpatāl (puṟam 31), tamiḻar aṟattaiyē ellāp pēṟukaṭkum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākum.
moḻittuṟaiyil, avar nākarikam aṭaintiruntatu pōṉṟu paṇpāṭum aṭaintiruntaṉar. oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkup pōtal eṉṟum kāl kaḻuvutal eṉṟum iṭakkaraṭakkiyum, iṟantavaṉait tuñciṉāṉ eṉṟum cāvaip perum piṟitu eṉṟum nalla pāmpu kaṭittalaik koṭit taṭṭal eṉṟum maṅkala vaḻakkākavum, kūṟivantaṉar.
periyōraik kai nīṭṭic cuṭṭāmalum, avarai nōkkik kāl nīṭṭāmalum, avar niṟka iruntu koṇṭu pēcāmalum, avarai nīṅkaḷ eṉṟu muṉṉilaip peyarāṟkuṟiyātu tāṅkaḷ eṉṟum aṅkuṟṟai eṉṟum paṭarkkaic collāṟ kuṟittum, paṇivuṭaimai kāṭṭi vantaṉar. potuvāka, iḻintōṉai nī eṉṟum, ottōṉai nīr eṉṟum. mūttōṉai nīṅkaḷ eṉṟum, uyarntōṉait tāṅkaḷ eṉṟum tuṟavumaṭat talaivaṉai aṅkuṟṟai eṉṟum, aracaṉaiyum muṉivaṉaiyum aṭikaḷ eṉṟum collāṟ cuṭṭuvatu tamiḻ marapākum.
teṉpulattār (iṟanta muṉṉōr) nāḷaṉṟu irappōrkkum ēḻai yeḷiyavarkkum āṇṭiyarkkum viruntaḷittum, iṟantupōṉa cūliyiṉ poruṭṭuc cumaitāṅkik kal naṭṭum, vēṉiṟ kālattil vaḻip
pōkkarkkut taṇṇīrp pantalum mōrp pantalum vaittum, āviṟku uriñcutaṟi naṭṭiyum, vilaṅkukaṭkut taṇṇīrt toṭṭi kaṭṭiyum, palavāṟu aṟañceytu vantaṉar.
	aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇa rōmpalum
	tuṟavōrk ketirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
	viruntetir kōṭalum 		                    (cilap.16:71-3)
illaṟattāral iyaṉṟavarai kaṭaip piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. mutaṟkāla antaṇar tamiḻa muṉivar.
vīṭu kaṭṭumpōtu, ayalārum āṇṭiyarum paṭuttuṟaṅkat terut tiṇṇai amaikkappeṟṟatu. māṭaṅkaḷiṉ ucciyil kāṭṭup puṟākkaḷ taṅkaṟkup puraikaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. cāraṅkaḷ iṭṭa tuḷaikaḷum kuruvikaḷ kūṭukaṭṭa viṭappaṭṭaṉa. kōyil maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭukaḷ vauvālkaḷ taṅkumāṟu araiyiruṭ ṭaṟaikaḷāy amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avai vauvāl natti eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ayal nāṭṭārum vaḻippōkkarum taṅku
vataṟku ūrār ūr maṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vaittaṉar.
uṭaikō vaṇamuṇṭuṟaṅkap puṟantiṇṇaiyuṇṭu
cakamuḻutum, paṭukkap puṟantiṇṇai yeṅkeṅkumuṇṭu
eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
	karuṅkaik kollaṉ irumpuvicait teṟinta
	kūṭat tiṇṇicai verīi māṭat
	tiṟaiyuṟai puṟaviṉ ceṅkāṟ cēval
	iṉṟuyil iriyum poṉtuñcu viyaṉakar
eṉpatu (perumpāṇ. 437-440), māṭap puṟāvaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
kūrai vīṭukaḷiṉ iṟappilum ciṭṭuk kuruvikaḷ taṅkum. itaṉāl, piṟappiṟappilē, eṉṟu ciṭṭukkuruvikkum civaperu māṉukkum iraṭṭuṟalākac coṉṉār kāḷamēkaṉār. makkaḷai nampi aṭaikkalam pukuvataṉālēyē, vīṭṭuk kuruvikku aṭaikkalāṉ eṉṟu peyar vantatu.
tiruvīḻimiḻalaiyil oru vauvāl natti maṇṭapam iṉṟum irukkiṉṟatu.
peṇṭir tam vīṭṭu muṟṟattiṟ kōla miṭuvataṟku aricu māvaip payaṉpaṭuttiyatu, eṟumpiṟku  uṇavātaṟ poruṭṭākum.
tamiḻar ellārum potuvāka aḵṟiṇaiyuyirikaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭi vantaṉar. vīṭṭil vaḷarppaṉavum mikappayaṉpaṭuvaṉavum kaṇṇiṟ kiṉiyaṉavumāṉa nilaittiṇai (tāvaram) ōṭuyiri paṟavai mutaliya
vaṟṟaip piḷḷaikaḷaip pōṉṟē pēṇiṉar. ataṉāl avaṟṟiṉ iḷamaikkup piḷḷaip peyar tōṉṟiyatōṭu, avaṟṟaip piḷḷaip pēṟillātavarkaḷ piḷḷaikaḷākavē karuti vaḷarkkum vaḻakkamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. irukkum piḷḷai mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷai mūṉṟu, paṟakkum piḷḷai mūṉṟu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. teṉṉam piḷḷai pōṉṟatu irukkum piḷḷai; kīrippiḷḷai pōṉṟatu ōṭum piḷḷai; kiḷip piḷḷai pōṉṟatu paṟakkum piḷḷai.
māṭu vaḷarppavarkaḷ, ciṟappāka uḻavarum iṭaiyarum, kāḷaikkum āviṟkum, muṟaiyē, cāttaṉ, cātti , muṭak koṟṟaṉ muṭakkoṟṟi, koṭum
puṟa marutaṉ koṭumpuṟa maruti mutaliya makkaṭ peyarkaḷaiyē iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. ataṉāl, avai irutiṇaikkum potuvāṉa viravupyer vakaiyāka ilakkaṇattilum iṭam peṟalāyiṉa. (tol col. 20-29)
poṅkaṟ paṇṭikaiyil māṭukaṭkum oru nāḷai otukki, avaṟṟiṟku aḻakiya aṇikaḷaip pūṭṭic ciṟanta uṇavūṭṭi māṭṭup poṅkal eṉak koṇṭāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
ellārum vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻar potu nōkkam.
	paciyum piṇiyum pakaiyum nīṅki
	vaciyum vaḷaṉum curakkeṉa vāḻtti 		    (maṇi. 2: 70-71)
viḻāt toṭaṅkuvatum,
	pāraka maṭaṅkalum pacippiṇi yaṟukeṉa
	ātirai yiṭṭaṉaḷ āruyir marunteṉ
eṉṉum maṇimēkalaik kūṟṟum (16: 134-5).
	ellārum iṉpuṟṟirukka niṉaippatuvē
	allāl vēṟoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parāparamē
eṉṉum tāyumāṉavar parāparak kaṇṇiyum (221) tamiḻariṉ potunala nōkkait teḷivāyk kāṭṭum.
piṟappāl ciṟappillai yeṉpatum, māntar ellārum ōriṉam eṉpatum paṇṭait tamiḻar koḷkaikaḷām. toḻil paṟṟiya vakuppē tamiḻar kulappirivākum.
	piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā
	ceytoḻil vēṟṟumai yāṉ. 		          (kuṟaḷ. 972)
	teṇkaṭal vaḷākam potumai yiṉṟi
	veṇkuṭai niḻaṟṟiya vorumai yōrkkum
	naṭunāḷ yāmattum pakalum tuñcāṉ
	kaṭumāp pārkkum kallā voruvaṟkum
	uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppavai iraṇṭē
	piṟavum ellām ōrok kummē
	celvattup payaṉē ītal
	tuyppēm eṉiṉē tappuna palavē.	 	             (puṟam.189)
	nalla kulameṉṟum tīya kulameṉṟum
	colvaḷa vallāṟ poruḷillai - tolciṟappiṉ
	oṇporuḷ oṉṟō tavam kalvi āḷviṉai
	eṉṟivaṟṟāṉ ākum kulam.  		          (nālaṭi.195)
	oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum          (tirumantiram , 2104)
	kulamum oṉṟē kuṭiyum oṉṟē	
	piṟappum oṉṟē iṟappum oṉṟē.                       (kapilar akaval)
tamiḻar kaṭaip piṭitta talaiyāya aṟaṅkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu, vāymaiyum, nērmaiyumākum, poy coṉṉa vāykkup pukāk kiṭaiyātu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. pukā = uṇavu.
māvali eṉṉum māperuñ cēra vēntaṉ, taṉakku iṟuti nērvatu terinta piṉṉum taṉvāyc col tappavillai.
ūravait tērtalil , iḷaiñar mutal mutiyōr varai ellārum kūṭiyuḷḷa maṇṭapattiṉ naṭuvil vaikkappaṭṭa kuṭattai, aṅkuḷḷa nampimāruḷ mutirntār oruvar eṭuttuk kaviḻttu ataṉuḷ oṉṟu
millaiyeṉṟu kāṭṭik kīḻē vaippar. ovvoru kuṭumpilum (Ward) takutiyuḷḷavar peyaraiyellām taṉittaṉi varainta olaikaḷai, kuṭumpiṉ peyar poṟitta vāyōlaiyuṭaṉ cērttukkaṭṭi, akkuṭattuḷ iṭuvar.  ataṉ piṉ, muṟkūṟiya mutu nampiyār aṅku naṭaipeṟuvatu iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyāta ciṟuvaṉaik koṇṭu  oru kaṭṭai eṭuppittu, atai aviḻttu vēṟoru kuṭattiliṭṭuk kulukki, avaṟṟuḷ ōr ōlaiyai acciṟuvaṉai eṭukkac colli vāṅki, aṅkuḷḷa kaṇakkaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuppar. avaṉ taṉ aintu viralaiyum akala virittu ataip peṟṟu, atiluḷḷa peyarai aṅkuḷḷār aṉaivarkkum kēṭkumāṟu urakkap paṭippāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, aṅkuḷḷa nampimār ellārum ov voruvarāy atai avvāṟē paṭippar. ataṉ piṉṉarē appeyar ēṭṭiṟ patiyap peṟum, iṅṅaṉam ellāk kuṭumpiṉiṉṟum terinteṭukkap peṟṟavarē ūravaiyuṟuppiṉarāvar.
ūravaik kaṇakkaṉ nalloḻukkamum nalvaḻiyil īṭṭiya poruḷum uṭaiyavaṉāyiruttal vēṇṭum. avaṉ avai yārkkuk kaṇakkuk kāṭṭumpōtu, paḻukkak kāycciya iruppu maḻuvaik kaiyil ēntik koṇṭu, tāṉ kāṭṭum kaṇakku uṇmaiyāṉa teṉṟu uṟuti kūṟavēṇṭum. avaṉ kai cuṭapṭāviṭiṉ, avaṉ āṭṭai naṉṉar (Annual Bonus) ākiya ēḻu kaḻañciṟkumēl kāṟpaṅku poṉ cērttuk koṭukkappeṟum; cuṭappaṭiṉ, pattuk kaḻañcu poṉ taṇṭamum vēṟu taṇṭaṉaiyum avaṉ aṭaivāṉ.
vaḻakku maṉṟaṅkaḷil, vaḻakkāḷikaḷ tam kūṟṟukkaḷaip paḻukkak kāycciya irumpaip piṭittal, naccup pāmpukkuṭattiṟkuḷ kaiviṭutal mutaliya teyvaccāṉṟu  koṇṭu meypikkum vaḻakkamum akkālattil iruntatu.
cīvakacintāmaṇi nūlāciriyarāṉa tiruttakkatēvar, paḻukkak kāycciya irumpuk kampiyai ēnti, tam kaṟpaip paṟṟip piṟarkkirunta aiyattai akaṟṟiṉār eṉṉum kataiyum, iṅkuk karutattakkatu. kaṟpuṭai makaḷir tam kaṟpai meypikka immuṟaiyaiyē perumpālum kaiyāṇṭaṉar.
paḻaiyaṉūr vēḷāḷar eḻupatiṉmar, avvūr vaṇikaṉukku uṟuti kūṟiyatai niṟaivēṟṟumāṟu taṟkolai ceytukoṇṭatu, ilakkiyap pukaḻpeṟṟa ceytiyākum.
tāḷāṇmai, vēḷāṇmai, taṉmāṉam, naṉṟi maṟavāmai eṉpaṉa tamiḻariṉ piṟa paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
	viṉaiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāḷnutal
	maṉaiyuṟai makaḷirk kāṭavar uyir   		           (kuṟun.135)
	vēḷāṇmai ceytu viruntōmpi veñcamattu
	vāḷāṇmai yālum valiyarāyt- tāḷāṇmai
	tāḻkkum maṭikōḷ ilarāy vāḻātār
	vāḻkkai tiruntuta liṉṟu  		    (paḻamoḻi.151)
	ennaṉṟi koṉṟārkkumuyvuṇṭām uyvillai
	ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku. 	                         (kuṟaḷ.110)
palvēṟu vakuppār paṇpāṭu
2. antaṇar paṇpāṭu :
(1) aiyaratu:
maṇapparuvam vantapiṉ āṇum peṇṇum tāmākak kūṭi vāḻnta mutaṟ kālattil, maṇakkavillai yeṉṟu poy colliyum, maṇantatāka oppukkoṇṭaviṭattum kaiviṭṭum, āṭavar peṇṭirkkut tīṅku ceytu vantatāl, avai perumpālum nērāvaṇṇam, palarkku muṉ uṟuti kūṟi maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷai maṇakkumāṟu, makkaḷ mītu aṉpum aruḷum koṇṭa irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōlum periyōr karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkai ēṟpaṭuttiṉar.
	poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
	aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.''  		       (tol. kaṟ.4)
aṟiyāmai ellāt tīmaikkum vērākaiyāl atai akaṟṟum poruṭṭup pala tuṟaikaḷil mutaṉūlai muṉivar aruḷic ceytaṉar.
	viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
	muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūl ākum''  	     (tol. mara. 95)
	uṭampār aḻiyil uyirāraḻivar
	tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉam cēravum māṭṭār
	uṭampiṉai vaḷarkkum upāyam aṟintē
	uṭampai vaḷarttēṉ uyirvaḷart tēṉē''
eṉṟu tirumūlar (tirumantiram,724) kūṟiyapaṭi, uṭampāṟ peṟakkūṭiya payaṉai muṟṟum peṟumāṟu vāḻnāḷai nīṭṭikkum maruttuvak kalaiyai, cittar kaṇṭu illaṟattārk kutavi yaruḷiṉar.
iṟaivaṉukku uṇmai aṟivu iṉpam mutaliya piṟa paṇpukaḷum vaṭivākumēṉum, aṉpē ciṟantatākak kaṇṭaṟintu iṟaivaṉaiyaṭaiya atuvē vaḻiyākak kūṟiyavar nāvalan tēyattil tamiḻa antaṇarē.
	aṉpum civamumiraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
	aṉpē civamāva tārumaṟikilār
	aṉpē civamāva tārumaṟintapiṉ	
	aṉpē civamāy amarntirun tārē.
eṉṟār tirumūlar (tirumantiram, 270).
	aṉpeṉum piṭiyu ḷakappaṭu malaiyē
		aṉpeṉuṅ kuṭipukum aracē
	aṉpeṉum valaikkuṭ paṭuparam poruḷē
		aṉpeṉum karattama ramutē
	aṉpeṉuṅ kaṭattu ḷaṭaṅkiṭuṅ kaṭalē
		aṉpeṉum uyiroḷir aṟivē
	aṉpeṉum aṇuvuḷ amaintapē roḷiyē
		aṉpuru vāmpara civamē 
eṉṉumirāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭṭum atuvē.
iṟaivaṉiṭattil aṭiyār ciṟṟiṉpattai vēṇṭuvatiṉum pēriṉpattai vēṇṭuvatē takka teṉpatai,
